However, most existing approaches are trained by distilling outputs from uncompressed RAG systems, inherently limiting their performance relative to the original model. To address this limitation, we propose DEX-Comp, a two-stage training recipe: Pure Distillation warm-starts the compression model on the uncompressed RAG's correct responses only, and Hard Exploration then runs reinforcement learning solely on queries the uncompressed RAG fails, forcing the model to explore computation patterns better suited to compressed representations. On five open-domain QA benchmarks at retrieval depths from top-5 to top-30, DEX-Comp compresses retrieved contexts by $16\times$ and accelerates inference by $4\times$--$24\times$, while achieving performance comparable to or exceeding the uncompressed RAG baseline across retrieval depths. Ablations and evaluations across diverse datasets and backbones further confirm the contribution of each stage and the generalization of our approach.

</details>


### [140] [Can Large Language Models Anticipate Behavioral Responses to Social Policies? A Case of Pension Enrollment Prediction among China's Flexible Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05189)
*Yumiao Li,Peixin Liu,Donglin Di,Chen Li,Runhuan Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexPension-LLM，首个面向中国灵活就业人员养老金参保预测任务的领域专用大语言模型，并结合DKI-RDistill方法注入政策规则线索进行知识蒸馏，在CHFS 2019盲测集上F1达0.9316，超越教师模型和多数基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会政策变革影响的评估对政策制定者是公认难题：计量经济学方法在推断假设情景时可能不可靠，而实地试点项目成本高昂。需要更高效可靠的政策评估工具。

Method: 提出使用大语言模型作为从通用模型适配而来的政策评估工具。构建FlexPension-LLM：1) 引入DKI-RDistill方法，向提示中注入政策引导线索（包括Probit边际效应和户籍省份养老金规则）；2) 使用LoRA/SFT将推理增强监督蒸馏到开源MoE学生模型中；3) 对教师错误案例在真实标签下重新生成以校正错误。

Result: 在CHFS 2019盲测集上Composite F1达0.9316，超越Claude Sonnet 4.5教师模型及17个基线中的15个，与Claude Opus 4.6统计上无显著差异。在四个外部调查中平均Composite F1为0.7549，且在最强势系统间性能波动范围最窄。

Conclusion: 增益主要来自政策引导线索注入和错误过滤监督，而推理过程提供可与政策规则对照核验的决策轨迹，表明LLM可作为有效且可解释的政策评估工具。

Abstract: Assessing the impacts of social policy changes is a widely acknowledged challenge for policymakers. Econometric methods can be unreliable when extrapolating to hypothetical scenarios, while field pilot programs are highly costly. In this paper, we propose using large language models (LLMs) as policy-assessment tools adapted from general-purpose models. We present FlexPension-LLM, the first domain-specialized large language model for a hierarchical pension-enrollment prediction task among flexible workers in China, and introduce DKI-RDistill, which injects policy-grounded cues into the prompt, including Probit-derived marginal effects and hukou-province pension rules. The method then uses LoRA/SFT to distill rationale-augmented supervision into an open-weight MoE student, with teacher errors corrected by regenerating those cases under ground-truth labels. On a CHFS 2019 blind split, FlexPension-LLM achieves 0.9316 Composite F1, surpassing its Claude Sonnet 4.5 teacher and 15 of 17 baselines, and is statistically indistinguishable from Claude Opus 4.6. Across four external surveys, it averages 0.7549 Composite F1 and shows the narrowest performance range among the strongest systems. Component analysis shows that gains come mainly from policy-grounded cue injection and error-filtered supervision, while rationales provide decision traces that can be checked against policy rules.

</details>


### [141] [A Verifier-Guided Explainable Reasoning Framework with Gold-Anchored QLoRA, Task-Aware Mixture-of-Experts, and Group-Relative RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05221)
*Thi Kim Trang Vo,Nam Tien Le,Thi Kim Nguyet Vo,Minh Khang Tran,Duy Phuong Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个验证器引导的可解释推理框架，用于透明的教育问答系统，结合了金标准锚定的QLoRA微调、任务感知符号路由和基于规则的强化学习（RLVR），在保持答案正确性的同时显著提升了推理深度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然具备较强的推理能力，但其生成的解释往往存在不一致性、缺乏依据或难以验证的问题，尤其在教育问答场景中，透明的、可验证的推理过程至关重要。

Method: 该框架包含三大核心组件：1）金标准锚定的QLoRA监督适配（使用权威答案和领域加权）；2）轻量级任务路由，将逻辑问题分配给FOL/Z3验证器，物理问题分配给公式和单位感知的符号求解器；3）验证器反馈驱动的组相对RLVR，从答案正确性（P1）、证据/单位一致性（P2）、推理深度与可解释性（P3）三个维度评估候选回答。推理时采用无需金标准的自洽性聚合，并结合可选的物理验证器进行系统级修正。

Result: 在438个保留测试样本上，RLVR将推理深度/可解释性（P3）从50.68%提升至72.20%；混合答案正确性（P1）保持在约55.94%。自洽性将纯模型P1从48.86%提升至50.23%，符号验证贡献了剩余的系统级提升。

Conclusion: RLVR主要增强了显式的推理结构，而符号验证在系统层面补充了神经策略，有效提升了答案的可靠性，两者互补共同构建了透明的教育问答系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong reasoning ability, but their explanations can remain inconsistent, weakly grounded, or difficult to verify. We propose a verifier-guided explainable reasoning framework for transparent educational question answering that combines gold-anchored QLoRA, task-aware symbolic routing, and group-relative RLVR. Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct is first adapted with field-weighted QLoRA supervision anchored to authoritative answers. A lightweight router then assigns logic problems to a FOL/Z3 verifier and physics problems to a formula- and unit aware symbolic solver. Verifier feedback is further used to support candidate evaluation, self-revision, and reward construction during RLVR. Candidate responses are evaluated along three complementary dimensions: P1 for answer correctness, P2 for evidence or unit consistency, and P3 for reasoning depth and explainability. At inference, gold-free self-consistency aggregates multiple candidate responses before an optional question-only physics verifier performs conservative system-level correction. On 438 held-out examples, RLVR increases P3 from 50.68% to 72.20%, while hybrid P1 remains approximately stable at 55.94%. Self-consistency improves model only P1 from 48.86% to 50.23%, with symbolic verification providing the remaining hybrid gain. These results indicate that RLVR primarily strengthens explicit reasoning structure, while symbolic verification complements the neural policy by improving answer reliability at the system level.

</details>


### [142] [Self-Supervised Lexical Representation Learning for Fast, Large-Scale Phylogenetic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05262)
*Tim Wientzek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全自监督的对比学习框架，直接从原始IPA转录词表中学习词汇表示，无需认知同源标注或专家输入，可在大规模数据集上高效推断系统发育树，并在3,399种语言变体上取得与Glottolog参考树竞争的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算系统发育学在历史语言学中至关重要，但全球尺度应用受限于两大瓶颈：基于字符的方法需要大量人工标注认知同源关系，以及大数据集推理的高昂计算成本。现有方法难以兼顾自动化和大规模效率。

Method: 提出自监督对比学习框架，包含双重对比目标：(1)词级损失，将语音相似的词组织形式化空间；(2)语言级辅助损失，使词汇空间反映语言的更广泛音系属性。从学习到的词表示推导语言两两距离，进而推断系统发育树。

Result: 在3,399种语言变体上推断的系统发育树，其广义四元组距离与Glottolog参考树相比具有竞争力的表现，且仅需在标准笔记本GPU上运行数分钟。词汇表示还能捕捉历时概念稳定性，其排名与既有排名显著相关。消融实验证实语言级目标和语音特征向量均改善了树拓扑质量。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模系统发育推断提供了计算高效、完全自动化的替代方案，并提供了统一的表示，可同时支持语言级和概念级的下游分析。

Abstract: Computational phylogenetics has become an essential tool in historical linguistics, yet its application at a global scale remains constrained by two factors: the labor-intensive manual annotation of cognacy judgments required for character-based methods and the substantial computational cost of inference on large datasets. This paper introduces a fully self-supervised contrastive learning framework that learns lexical representations directly from raw IPA-transcribed wordlists, without requiring cognacy annotations, alignments, or additional expert input. The model employs a dual contrastive objective: a word-level loss that organizes phonetically similar forms into a coherent space, and an auxiliary language-level loss that encourages the lexical space to reflect broader phonological properties of languages. From the resulting word representations, pairwise language distances are derived and used to infer a global phylogenetic tree of 3,399 language varieties. The inferred tree achieves a generalized quartet distance (GQD) to the Glottolog reference tree competitive with multiple baselines, while requiring only minutes of computation on a standard notebook GPU. Furthermore, the same representations capture diachronic concept stability: variance in pairwise distances across languages yields stability rankings that correlate significantly with established rankings. Ablation studies confirm that both the language-level objective and the use of phonetic feature vectors improved the inferred trees topology with regards to GQD. The framework thus provides a computationally efficient and fully automatic alternative for large-scale phylogenetic inference and offers a unified representation supporting downstream analyses at both the language and concept level.

</details>


### [143] [LexFlip: A Dissociation Diagnostic for Legal Meaning Preservation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05296)
*Gaurab Baral*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有法律文本语义一致性检查方法的缺陷，并提出"分离项"（dissociation）来解耦表面形式与法律效力。作者发布了LexFlip数据集（373个魁北克法语法律条文的极小扰动，保留93%词元却反转法律效力），并配套测试框架。实验显示嵌入模型和BERTScore在此类编辑上仅使用其0.022-0.039的判别范围（远低于双向NLI的0.670），且一个简单的长度特征在FrJudge上超越了所有语义指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律条款改写后的一致性检查无法真正验证简化条文是否保留了原意——因为"相同对得分最高、无关对得分最低"的检验标准使词汇重叠与法律效力同步移动，任何token重叠的单调函数都能同时满足这两个条件。

Method: 提出"分离项"（dissociation）方法：固定表面形式的同时改变法律效力。构建LexFlip数据集，包含373个反转法律效力但保留93%词元的魁北克法语法律条文扰动。建立统一测试框架，对嵌入类度量、回归模型和提示式评判器（judges）进行评测。

Result: 7种嵌入和BERTScore度量在反转法律效力的编辑上仅使用其"相同-无关"判别范围的0.022到0.039，而双向NLI达到0.670（同类检验中唯一不合格的家族）。在FrJudge上，简单长度特征的表现超过了所有语义度量，且与测得的人类上限r=0.597相比有最低的差距。

Conclusion: 现有文本相似度检验方法在检测法律效力的反转上严重不足，需要新的评测基准和度量设计来真正验证法律文本简化后是否保持了语义一致性。

Abstract: Does a simplified legal clause still say what the original said? The checks in current use cannot establish that it does: requiring an identical pair to score highest and an unrelated pair lowest moves lexical overlap and legal force together, so any monotone function of token overlap satisfies both. Our remedy is a dissociation, an item holding surface form fixed while legal force moves. We release LexFlip, 373 minimal perturbations of Quebec statutory French that reverse legal force while preserving 0.93 of the tokens, with a harness scoring metrics, regressors and prompted judges alike. The seven embedding and BERTScore metrics we test spend only 0.022 to 0.039 of their identical-to-unrelated range on such an edit, against 0.670 for bidirectional NLI, the one family the identical-pair check would disqualify. On FrJudge, against a measured human ceiling of r=0.597, a bare length feature outscores every semantic metric and has the lowest margin we measure.

</details>


### [144] [WearableQA: A Benchmark for Health Reasoning over Real-World Wearable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05405)
*Ji Soo Lee,Xilun Chen,Pierce Chuang,Ashish Shenoy,Jason Wei,Dohwan Ko,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Benoit Corda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍WearableQA，一个基于200名真实用户长达500天可穿戴设备数据的基准测试，包含4,084道10选1多选题，用于评估LLM对真实纵向可穿戴数据（时间序列、血液生物标志物、人口统计信息）的推理能力。通过16种问题类型沿数据vs健康推理和单信号vs跨信号推理两个维度设计，采用文献+统计双重验证框架构建问题。评估14个模型性能为19.6%-72.9%，大部分低于60%，说明该基准远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴传感基准很少评估AI系统是否能在真实用户的长期可穿戴记录上进行推理，缺乏能保留设备噪声和个体差异的真实分布数据集，亟需一个可靠且能区分不同推理能力的诊断性基准。

Method: 从200名真实用户的可穿戴时间序列、血液生物标志物和人口统计数据构建4,084道10选项多选题；引入16种问题类型，沿两条互补轴组织（数据vs健康推理、单信号vs跨信号推理）；采用双重验证框架，结合文献依据的生理学发现与统计验证的人群生理模式来生成问题。

Result: 评估14个专有和开源LLM，模型表现从19.6%到72.9%不等（10%随机基线），有效区分模型能力；大多数模型准确率低于60%，表明WearableQA仍远未被解决。

Conclusion: WearableQA是一个现实且具诊断性的基准，能够有效评估LLM对真实世界可穿戴数据的推理能力，适合作为该领域持续研究的评测平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in wearable sensing enable continuous monitoring of physiological and behavioral signals, yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether AI systems can reason over a real user's longitudinal wearable record. We introduce WearableQA, a benchmark comprising 4,084 10-option multiple-choice questions constructed from the wearable time series, blood biomarkers, and demographics of 200 real users, each with up to 500 days of daily measurements. WearableQA preserves authentic wearable distributions that include device noise and inter-individual variability. To evaluate distinct reasoning capabilities, we introduce 16 question types organized along two complementary axes: data versus health reasoning, which distinguishes computation over longitudinal measurements from physiological interpretation; and single- versus cross-signal reasoning, which separates reasoning about individual signals from the integration of multiple signals. To construct reliable questions at scale, we adopt a dual-grounding framework that combines literature-grounded physiological findings with statistically validated population-grounded physiological patterns. This enables the capture of meaningful relationships observed in real-world wearable data. Evaluation of 14 proprietary and open-source LLMs demonstrates that WearableQA effectively differentiates model capabilities, with performance ranging from 19.6% to 72.9% against a 10% chance baseline. Moreover, WearableQA remains far from solved: most models achieve accuracies below 60%. Overall, WearableQA provides a realistic and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over real-world wearable data.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [At Equal Inference Cost, Multi-Agent Structure Does Not Beat a Single Frozen Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04217)
*David Dylan,Aoife Brennan,Cian Murphy,Niamh O'Sullivan,Conor Kelly,Saoirse Walsh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-Evolve方法，在固定语言模型调用总数（等量推理预算）条件下，对比进化多智能体团队（Planner-Executor-Critic）与进化单一智能体的性能。实验表明，在ALFWorld上完整团队（0.769）与单一执行者（0.754）无显著差异（p=0.80），团队价值完全来自执行者，规划者和评论者角色退化；在WebShop上团队表现更差。结论是等成本下多智能体结构增加开销而无明显收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究比较多智能体团队与单一智能体时，通常给予团队更多环境回滚次数（即额外计算），导致对比不公平。作者希望固定总推理调用次数，公平检验多智能体团队在相同预算下是否真的优于单一智能体。

Method: 提出MA-Evolve框架：将Planner-Executor-Critic团队表示为三个可进化的角色提示，在共享冻结的7B骨干模型上对每个角色进行坐标上升优化。通过消融分析（leave-one-in）区分各角色贡献，并在ALFWorld和WebShop两个基准上，在等量以及2-3倍推理调用预算下对比评估。

Result: ALFWorld上：单一执行者进化显著优于未进化智能体；完整团队均值最高（0.769 vs 0.754）但统计上无显著差异（p=0.80），且消耗1.8倍评估调用。Leave-one-in分析表明价值完全来自执行者，规划者和评论者进化为空提示或低影响提示。给定2-3倍免费计算，团队仅匹敌单一智能体；WebShop上进化无效且团队趋势更差。

Conclusion: 在等量推理成本下，多智能体结构（规划器、评论者等辅助角色）对中小型LLM带来的额外开销无法用收益抵消。团队报道的性能优势可能源于给予额外计算而非角色分工本身的价值。研究者应重新审视多智能体方法中角色分配的真正贡献。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines, such as Planner-Executor-Critic teams, often report gains over single agents, but these gains usually come with higher inference cost because the team makes multiple model calls per environment step. Existing automated methods search over roles, topologies, and prompts, but typically compare teams against single agents at equal environment rollouts, giving the team extra compute. We instead fix the total number of language-model calls and ask whether evolving a multi-agent team still beats evolving a single agent under the same budget.
  We introduce MA-Evolve, which represents a Planner-Executor-Critic team as three evolvable role prompts and optimizes them by per-role coordinate ascent over a shared frozen 7B backbone. On ALFWorld, evolving a single executor significantly improves over the unevolved agent, while the full team achieves the highest mean but is not statistically better than the single agent: 0.769 versus 0.754, p = 0.80, despite using 1.8 times more evaluation calls. Leave-one-in analysis shows that the realized value comes entirely from the executor; the planner and critic evolve to empty or low-impact prompts and rarely change the executor's action. With 2-3 times free compute, the team only matches the single agent, and on WebShop evolution is null while the team trends worse. Under equal inference cost, multi-agent structure adds cost without clear benefit.

</details>


### [146] [Multi-dimensional Bias in Modeling Multi-dimensional Preferences: Evaluating the Ability of Synthetic Agents to Replace Human Participants in Conjoint Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04243)
*Ho Ting Hung,Nachiket Midha,Victor Y. Wu,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文考察合成智能体能否在联合分析（conjoint）设计中复现人类多维偏好模式，通过复刻已发表的联合分析研究，从表征对应性、推断对应性和程序稳定性三个维度对比合成数据与真人数据，发现结果在各维度及研究间参差不齐，表明合成参与者的有效性应被视为"主张依赖"和分层的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在调查实验中用于增强稳健性或降低数据采集成本日益受到关注，但它们在联合分析设计中的有效性尚未被充分探索，而联合分析是政治学中越来越流行的方法。

Method: 复刻已发表的联合分析研究，将合成智能体生成的结果与原始人类数据进行比较，分析沿三个维度展开：表征对应性（选择分布的对齐程度）、推断对应性（估计的统计与实质性相似度）、程序稳定性。

Result: 结果在不同维度和不同复刻研究之间表现不均一（uneven）。虽然在某些情况下能复现图形或获得强符号一致性，但这仅表明聚合输出相似，不足以支持用合成参与者替代人类受访者。

Conclusion: 合成参与者的有效性应被视为"主张依赖"和分层的（claim-dependent and hierarchical）；学科整体须先在不同层面绘制这一创新的边界，才能将合成智能体视为人类样本的可靠替代品。

Abstract: Despite growing interest in using LLMs to add robustness or reduce data-collection costs in survey experiments, their efficacy in conjoint design---an increasingly popular method in political science---remains underexplored. This paper addresses that gap by investigating whether synthetic agents can reproduce the multi-dimensional human preference patterns that conjoint is designed to capture. It replicates published conjoint studies and compares the results generated by synthetic agents with original human data along three dimensions: representational correspondence, inferential correspondence, and procedural stability. Our analysis evaluates the alignment of choice distributions as well as the statistical and substantive similarity of estimates, and the results are uneven across these dimensions and studies replicated. This implies that the validity of synthetic participants should be considered claim-dependent and hierarchical. Reproducing a figure or obtaining strong sign agreement is evidence of similar aggregate outputs, but not enough to support replacing human respondents. Our results suggest that the discipline as a whole must first map this innovation's boundaries across various levels before considering synthetic agents a robust substitute for human samples.

</details>


### [147] [EVOHARNESSBENCH: Can Your Agents Keep Pace with an Evolving Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04280)
*Zixuan Ke,Vaidehi Patil,Haizhou Shi,Yang Li,Ye Liu,Sarath Shekkizhar,Anurag Koul,Jiayu Wang,Xuan Phi Nguyen,Semih Yavuz,Mohit Bansal,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOHARNESSBENCH基准，专门评估LLM智能体在工具、技能、智能体三个维度的"外挂系统（harness）"持续演化场景下的表现，发现外挂扩展会导致智能体对已会任务的性能退化（外挂诱导遗忘），且保留旧能力与适应新能力之间存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体的能力高度依赖于外部外挂系统（工具、可复用技能、专家智能体），而该外挂在实际应用中持续演化。现有持续学习基准都把非平稳性放在任务流上而保持外挂固定，缺乏专门评估外挂自身演化对智能体影响的研究，因此需要建立这样一个基准来揭示这一被忽视的挑战。

Method: 构建了包含17个多阶段外挂演化流、802个任务、520个工具、42个技能和62个智能体的基准EVOHARNESSBENCH，基于可验证的基准确定性生成。设计两个互补评估设置：部署评估（隔离外挂扩展时对既有能力的保留）和自我演化适应评估（测试新能力引入时累积经验是否仍有用）。

Result: 揭示了三个持续性缺口：(1) 单纯外挂扩展就能使已解决任务的性能下降，产生外挂诱导遗忘；(2) 自我演化适应的收益在不同演化阶段、能力轴和环境间不稳定；(3) 保留旧能力与适应新能力可能互相矛盾，保留早期能力并不必然改善对新能力的适应，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 外挂演化构成了智能体构建中的独特挑战：智能体需要既能跟上持续演化的外挂，又能保持此前有效的行为。该基准的发现为设计能够适应动态外挂的智能体提供了重要的评估基础。

Abstract: Modern LLM-based agents operate through a harness of tools, reusable skills, and specialist agents that shapes what they observe and what they can do. In practice, this harness continually evolves as new capabilities are added. We introduce EVOHARNESSBENCH, a benchmark for evaluating agents under controlled harness evolution across three axes (tools, skills, and agents). Unlike existing continual-learning benchmarks for agents, which typically place non-stationarity (i.e., what changes over time) in the task stream while keeping the harness fixed, EVOHARNESSBENCH places non-stationarity in the externally supplied harness itself. It contains 17 multi-stage harness streams constructed deterministically from verifier-based benchmarks, comprising 802 tasks, 520 tools, 42 skills, and 62 agents. We evaluate two complementary settings corresponding to the central challenges of harness evolution: deployment evaluation, which isolates retention of previously accessible competence as the harness expands, and self-evolving adaptation evaluation, which tests whether accumulated experience remains useful as new capabilities are introduced. Our results reveal three persistent gaps. First, harness expansion alone can degrade performance on previously solved tasks, producing harness-induced forgetting. Second, gains from self-evolving adaptation remain inconsistent across stages of harness evolution, capability axes, and environments. Third, retention and adaptation can pull in different directions: preserving earlier competence does not necessarily improve adaptation to newly introduced capabilities, and vice versa. These results establish harness evolution as a distinct challenge for building agents that can keep pace with an evolving harness while preserving previously effective behavior.

</details>


### [148] [Abstraction Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04303)
*Boning Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Abstraction Agent，一种零样本流水线，利用大语言模型从自然语言游戏描述中自动发现战略特征、对私有状态进行评分并聚类生成抽象桶，无需领域专家评估器或训练数据。在HUNL转牌局中相对期望手牌强度基线将可利用性降低多达62%，并能零成本迁移到多种未训练游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息抽象是扩展大型不完备信息博弈求解算法的关键，但传统上构建有效抽象需要领域特定的评估器（如手牌强度计算器或权益估计器），这些需要专家知识和工程投入，且对大多数研究较少的游戏不可用，限制了抽象方法在更广泛游戏上的适用性。

Method: 提出四阶段零样本流水线：(1) 特征发现与校准锚点，由LLM从自然语言规则中提取连续战略特征；(2) 批量私有状态评分；(3) 基于相关性的特征选择；(4) k-means聚类形成抽象桶。整个过程无需任何游戏特定评估器、训练数据或博弈树遍历。

Result: 在HUNL转牌局的头对头局面上，相对期望手牌强度基线降低了最多62%的提升策略可利用性；在ROVER Trials（一个不存在于任何预训练语料库的原创游戏）上，在所有粒度下均优于标量排名基线；并零改动迁移到四张牌底池限注奥马哈、HUNL翻牌前/翻牌及立直麻将。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了结构化知识抽取——将LLM参数中的隐式战略知识转化为显式数值特征用于下游算法计算。代码已开源。

Abstract: Information abstraction, which groups strategically similar private states into a tractable number of buckets, is essential for scaling game-solving algorithms to large imperfect-information games. Constructing effective abstractions, however, has traditionally required domain-specific evaluators such as hand-strength calculators or equity estimators, which demand expert knowledge and engineering effort and are unavailable for most less-studied games. We propose the Abstraction Agent, a zero-shot pipeline that uses a large language model (LLM) to discover continuous strategic features from a natural-language game description, score private states on these features, and cluster them into abstraction buckets, without any game-specific evaluator, training data, or game-tree traversal during abstraction construction. The pipeline runs in four phases: feature discovery with calibration anchors, batched private-state scoring, correlation-based feature selection, and $k$-means clustering. The resulting abstractions reduce lifted-strategy exploitability by up to 62% relative to an expected-hand-strength baseline on heads-up no-limit Texas hold'em (HUNL) turn endgames, and beat a scalar rank baseline at every granularity on ROVER Trials, an original game absent from any pretraining corpus. Beyond these quantitative benchmarks, the pipeline transfers with unchanged prompts to four-card Pot-Limit Omaha, HUNL preflop and flop, and Riichi Mahjong, where the discovered features track each game's recognized strategic concepts. This is structured knowledge elicitation: converting implicit strategic knowledge in LLM parameters into explicit numerical features for downstream algorithmic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/lbn187/AbstractionAgent.

</details>


### [149] [Online Change-point Detection for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05298)
*Fatemeh Saberi Khomami,Julita Vassileva*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Patterns of Past Rewards (PPR)，一种用于合作式多智能体强化学习（MARL）的轻量级在线变点检测方法。PPR通过对智能体的回报序列进行平滑处理并应用统计漂移检测器来识别训练过程中的显著变化，在检测速度与告警稳定性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作式MARL系统依赖历史经验学习协调行为，但环境或任务目标在训练中发生变化时，这些经验可能变得不可靠。智能体需要先识别情境变化才能决定如何适应，因此需要一种在线变点检测方法。

Method: PPR（Patterns of Past Rewards）是一种轻量级、与算法无关的检测器。它首先对智能体的回报流进行平滑处理以突出近期变化，然后应用统计漂移检测器标记显著的转变。该方法在基于Multi-Agent Particle Environment的Speaker-Listener环境中，在两类受控非平稳场景下进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，检测速度与告警稳定性之间存在权衡：基于平滑回报的基线方法检测更早但产生大量重复告警；直接将检测器应用于原始回报则经常遗漏变化。PPR能够在限制冗余检测的同时识别受控的变化，提供了更均衡的方案。

Conclusion: PPR是一种轻量级、基于回报的监控工具，能够使合作式MARL系统在训练过程中可靠地识别主要变化，在检测性能与告警可靠性之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems rely on past experience for learning coordinated behaviour, but this experience may become unreliable if the environment or task objective changes during training. In such cases, agents first need a way to recognize that the situation has changed before deciding how to adapt. This paper studies online change-point detection for cooperative MARL using reward-derived signals. We propose \emph{Patterns of Past Rewards} (PPR), a lightweight algorithm-agnostic detector that smooths agents' return streams, highlights recent changes, and applies a statistical drift detector to flag significant shifts. We evaluate PPR in a custom Speaker-Listener environment based on the Multi-Agent Particle Environment under two controlled non-stationarity scenarios. Our results show a trade-off between detection speed and alarm stability. A smoothed-return baseline detects earlier but produces many repeated alarms. In contrast, applying the detector directly to raw returns often misses the shift. PPR offers a more balanced approach by limiting redundant detections while still identifying the controlled shifts. These findings highlight PPR as a lightweight, reward-based monitoring tool that enables cooperative MARL systems to reliably identify major changes during training.

</details>


### [150] [Trust-Aware Adaptive Disclosure for Inference Privacy Preservation in Multi-Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05340)
*Puspanjali Ghoshal,Tobias J. Oechtering*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信任感知的隐私控制框架（Trust-Aware Privacy Control），用于多智能体系统在目标推断攻击下的隐私保护，通过信任相关的随机策略控制信息发布，实现共识性能与隐私保护的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统被广泛应用于医疗管理和智能电网等关键信息系统中，每个智能体拥有需要对外部对手保密的潜在目标。然而，现有的多智能体共识协议容易受到目标推断攻击，对手可通过观察信息流推断智能体的隐藏目标，因此需要设计有效的隐私保护机制。

Method: 该方法提出一个信任感知的隐私控制框架，核心是：1) 基于智能体之间的动态信任关系来调整消息披露策略；2) 通过信任相关的随机策略控制信息发布；3) 在共识性能与隐私保护之间实现可调节的权衡。

Result: 实验表明，该方法相比代表性基线方法能有效降低对手的目标推断准确率，同时保持具有竞争力的共识效用，证明了信任感知机制在多智能体系统隐私保护中的有效性。

Conclusion: 信任感知的隐私控制机制能够在多智能体系统中实现隐私保护与共识性能的良好平衡，为信息关键系统中的多智能体隐私安全提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Agent based systems are increasingly deployed in information critical systems including healthcare management systems, and smart grids. In this paper, we consider a multi-agent system where each agent has a latent goal that needs to be kept hidden from observing adversaries. More specifically, this paper studies privacy-preserving consensus in networked multi-agent systems under goal inference attacks. We propose a Trust-Aware Privacy Control framework that adapts message disclosure based on the dynamic trust relationships between agents. The proposed method controls information release using a trust-dependent stochastic policy. This enables a tradeoff between consensus performance and privacy preservation. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method reduces adversarial goal inference accuracy compared to representative baselines, while maintaining competitive consensus utility, thereby highlighting the effectiveness of trust-aware mechanisms in privacy preservation of the agents in multi-agent systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Blockchain-Enabled Secure Logging for Fiscal Electronic Mechanisms: Evaluation of the Greek eSEND and myDATA Tax Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04356)
*Panagiotis Mavridis,Anargyros Baklezos,Christos Nikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了希腊财政电子机制（FEMs）中基于区块链的完整性机制与集中式税务信息系统eSEND的架构，并将其与电子发票服务提供商和myDATA平台进行对比。研究发现，基于硬件的财政机制在交易完整性和防篡改方面优于纯软件发票基础设施，并指出了当前电子发票框架的架构弱点及改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在数字税务生态系统中，硬件型财政设备与纯软件型电子发票基础设施之间在交易完整性保障上的差异，揭示当前电子发票框架中缺乏区块链完整性保证的架构弱点。

Method: 对希腊各类财政设备（电子收银机、财政打印机、财政签名机、FEMAS设备）的双重或三重哈希链加密架构进行分析，评估财政设备与中央数据库之间传输协议的加密、顺序验证和区块链验证机制，并与基于软件的电子发票系统架构进行对比研究。

Result: 研究表明硬件型财政机制（FEMs）在交易完备性和防篡改能力上强于纯软件型发票基础设施，而当前电子发票框架（如myDATA）在架构上缺乏区块链完整性和交易防篡改的保证机制。

Conclusion: 当前电子发票基础设施存在架构性弱点，建议借鉴硬件财政机制中的区块链完整性方法，以提升数字税务生态系统中的交易完整性保障。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the implementation of blockchain-based integrity mechanisms in Greek Fiscal Electronic Mechanisms (FEMs) and the central tax information system eSEND. The study examines the cryptographic architecture of fiscal devices, including Electronic Cash Registers, Fiscal Printers, Fiscal Signing Machines, and FEMAS devices, which implement double or triple hash-chain structures to ensure transaction immutability. The transmission protocol between fiscal devices and the central database is also evaluated with respect to encryption, sequential validation, and blockchain verification. In contrast, the architecture of Electronic Invoicing Provider Services and the myDATA central platform is analyzed, highlighting the absence of blockchain-based integrity guarantees. The comparison demonstrates that hardware-based fiscal mechanisms provide stronger guarantees for transaction completeness and tamper resistance than purely software-based invoicing infrastructures. The findings highlight architectural weaknesses in the current e-invoicing framework and propose improvements for ensuring transaction integrity in digital tax ecosystems.

</details>


### [152] [Privacy Failure in Split-LLM Training, The Returned Gradient Nullifies the Decoys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04382)
*Georgios Politis,Evangelos Pappas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一个两节点分割LLM训练系统进行了系统安全案例研究，发现其隐私评估虽然通过，但存在一个未被测试的观测通道：受信任本地节点发送的帧中混合了真实行与诱饵行，诱饵行的梯度恰好为零，零的图案泄露了哪些行是真实的。实验在九种随机种子下，每帧4096行全部被识别，成功率100%；攻击帧内容比常数猜测基线多恢复约每百个token一个（+0.65到+1.50个百分点），而打乱标签的对照实验未恢复任何信息。在保持模型质量预算内的配置上同样复现了该发现。所有运行都通过了前向通道隐私检查和质量检查，但将返回梯度纳入后均未通过同一隐私检查。对梯度每行进行裁剪和加噪后，以约0.01 nats的交叉熵损失封闭了该泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私评估方法可能遗漏未被测试的观测通道。该研究旨在表明，即使系统通过了前向通道隐私评估和质量检查，仍可能因忽略返回梯度中零元素的图案而存在实际可利用的信息泄漏，揭示隐私评估框架的系统性盲区。

Method: 采用两节点分割LLM训练架构（TLN发送保护激活给UCN，UCN返回输出，TLN持有私有损失并返回输出梯度）。帧混合真实行与诱饵行，诱饵行梯度恰好为零。采用预先固定的协议：以已知强度注入泄漏以证明仪器敏感性，使用打乱标签对照证明无虚假报告，在运行前设定阈值。在九种随机种子上进行多次运行，并包括保持模型质量预算内的第二组配置。

Result: （1）九种随机种子下，零图案在每帧、每次运行共4096/4096行中全部正确识别真实行；（2）帧内容攻击比常数猜测基线多恢复约每百个token一个（+0.65至+1.50个百分点）；（3）打乱标签对照实验未恢复任何信息；（4）所有运行均通过前向通道隐私检查和质量检查，但加入返回梯度后均失败；（5）梯度每行裁剪加噪以约0.01 nats交叉熵代价封闭泄漏。

Conclusion: 该研究证明分割LLM训练的隐私评估存在重大盲区：返回梯度中零图案构成一个可观测且可利用的泄漏通道，且常规前向通道检查无法发现。虽然行级裁剪加噪可封闭该泄漏且代价很小，但系统并非安全——包括跨训练步骤累积观测在内的五类攻击从未被测量，说明隐私评估需要更全面的威胁建模。

Abstract: We present a systems-security case study of a two-node split-LLM training system whose privacy evaluation passed while leaving an observable channel untested. The Trusted Local Node (TLN) sends protected activations to the Untrusted Cloud Node (UCN), the UCN returns its output, and TLN, holding the private loss, returns the output gradient. The frame the UCN receives mixes real rows with decoys, and the loss ignores the decoys. Their gradients are exactly zero, so the pattern of zeros reveals which rows were real. We measure it with a protocol fixed in advance: a leak injected at known strength to prove the instrument can see one, a shuffled-label control to prove it does not report absent leaks, and a threshold set before the runs. Across nine seeds, the zeros identified the real rows on every frame, 4,096 of 4,096 per run. An attack on the frame contents recovered about one extra token per hundred over a constant-guess baseline (+0.65 to +1.50 percentage points); the shuffled controls recovered nothing. A second set of runs repeated this on a configuration that keeps model quality within budget, so the finding is not confined to a setting nobody would deploy. On both datasets, every such run passed the forward-channel privacy check and the quality check, yet failed that same check once the returned gradient was included. Clipping and noising each row of the gradient closed the leak for about 0.01 nats of held-out cross-entropy. The system is not thereby safe: five classes of attack, including those accumulating observations across training steps, were never measured.

</details>


### [153] [Candidate Comparability Before Promotion: Conditional Validation in Adaptive Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04388)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Amaia Pikatza-Huerga,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应网络入侵检测系统中"分类器提升（promotion）"评估的方法论问题，发现提升结论高度依赖挑战者构建方式与证据量，提出应显式控制、报告和解释这些因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移告警只能检测变化，不能证明挑战者分类器应替换已部署的分类器。而提升决策具有安全相关性，但评估提升存在方法论缺陷：结论可能依赖于挑战者的构建方式和支撑证据的多少。

Method: 在CICIDS2017、UNSW-NB15和ToN-IoT三个基准数据集上，使用自包含挑战者流水线、嵌套候选规模控制、九种更新策略的公共测试平台比较，以及将每个精确特征向量限定于单一评估/训练/探测角色的敏感性分析。

Result: 原有分类器自有的冻结预处理放大了表面上的提升危害；自包含挑战者流水线下全漂移危害不持续。名义候选证据从512提至每类2,000样本使提升改善+0.53/+1.67/+0.38平衡准确率点，在三个基准上均为正向且统计显著，但基准依赖性强；策略排序随候选可比性变化，无全局占优策略，标签无关估计器与校准集成的兼容性结论收窄。

Conclusion: 提升评估中必须显式控制、报告并解释挑战者构建方式和证据量；验证对证据劣势的挑战者有助益，但在证据相当情况下无平均收益；真实时间序列流量的重放实验中，总是部署也未造成净危害。

Abstract: Adaptive network intrusion detection systems retrain classifiers after drift alarms, but an alarm detects change; it does not establish that a challenger should replace the deployed incumbent. Promotion is security-relevant because it changes the model responsible for subsequent attack detection, and evaluating it has a methodological problem: promotion conclusions may depend on how the challenger was constructed and on how much evidence supports it. We test that dependence on CICIDS2017, UNSW-NB15 and ToN-IoT with self-contained challenger pipelines, nested candidate-size controls, a common-harness comparison of nine update policies, and a final sensitivity confining every exact feature vector to one evaluation, training or probe role. Incumbent-owned frozen preprocessing amplified apparent promotion harm; with self-contained challenger pipelines the mean full-drift harm did not persist. Raising nominal candidate evidence from 512 to 2,000 samples per class improved promotion under pool-constructed progressive drift by +0.53, +1.67 and +0.38 balanced-accuracy points: positive and statistically resolved in all three benchmarks, but materially benchmark-dependent rather than homogeneous, and driven mainly by fewer false positives. Policy conclusions were partially robust: policy ordering changed with candidate comparability, no policy globally dominated, and earlier compatibility statements for a label-free estimator and a calibrated ensemble narrowed. Validation helped evidence-disadvantaged challengers but added no average benefit at parity. Thirteen replays on real, time-ordered traffic showed no net harm from always deploying. Challenger construction and evidence should be controlled, reported and interpreted explicitly when promotion is evaluated.

</details>


### [154] [Engineered Persuasion: Evaluating Personalized Pretexts in LLM-Generated Spear Phishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04410)
*Jerson Francia,Derek Hansen,Benjamin Schooley,Shydra Valynn Murray*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过180名美国在职成年人对AI生成的钓鱼邮件进行评价实验，发现邮件个性化程度每提升一级，可信度评分增加2.40分，点击意图概率增加28%，但细节是否与收件人实际工作情境匹配才是关键——匹配的细节增强可信度，错误或不恰当的细节反而引起怀疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可以低成本地将工作场所细节插入钓鱼邮件中，但这些细节可能增强或削弱信息的可信度。现有研究缺乏对个性化程度如何系统性影响钓鱼邮件说服力的量化分析。

Method: 招募180名美国在职成年人对AI生成的钓鱼邮件进行公开调查评估。邮件包含四个累计个人信息层级（工作场所、收件人姓名与职位、工作职责、同事/共享项目背景）。参与者对每封邮件的可信度进行0-100评分，选择指定行动（打开链接、调查、删除或报告），并解释最高和最低评分邮件的理由。共收集1,436份有效评价。

Result: 在敏感性分析中，每个个性化层级使可信度评分增加2.40分，点击意图的几率每层级增加28%。在未表达点击意图的参与者中，调查行为仍常见、报告行为下降、删除行为增加。事后描述性分析发现，来自具名人员并引用同事的邮件比来自部门或实体的邮件获得更高的评分和点击意图。定性编码显示细节匹配收件人角色和日常事务时增强可信度，而不正确、模糊或渠道不合适的细节会引发怀疑。

Conclusion: 钓鱼邮件的个性化并非简单堆砌更多细节，关键在于前提是否契合收件人的工作情境。这一发现可为工作场所网络安全培训提供指导。

Abstract: Large language models can insert workplace details into phishing pretexts at low cost, but those details may either support or undermine a message's credibility. We recruited 180 U.S. working adults to evaluate simulated, AI-generated phishing emails in a disclosed survey. The emails used four cumulative levels of information: workplace (Level 1); recipient name and job title; job responsibilities; and coworker/shared-project context (Level 4). Participants rated each message's convincingness from 0 to 100, chose one stated action (open the link, investigate, delete, or report), and explained why their highest- and lowest-rated messages stood out. Across 1,436 valid evaluations, convincingness increased by 2.40 points per personalization level in a sensitivity analysis, while the odds of expressing click intention increased by 28\% per level. Among participants who did not express an intention to click, investigation remained common, reporting declined, and deletion increased. A post-hoc descriptive analysis found higher ratings and click intention for messages from a named person who referenced a supplied coworker than for messages from a department or entity. Qualitative coding showed why added detail could help or hurt: details that matched participants' roles and routines supported credibility, while incorrect, vague, or channel-inappropriate details raised suspicion. Together, the results highlight that personalization is not simply a matter of adding more details: it depends on whether the pretext fits the recipient's work context. We discuss how this distinction can inform workplace cybersecurity training.

</details>


### [155] [Nebulon Enterprise Simulated Threats for Phishing Research (NEST-Phish): A Synthetic Enterprise Phishing Email Dataset for Behavioral and Machine-Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04474)
*Emily J. Winokur,Lauren S. Treiman,Allen G. Moore,Paul Schutte,Joe Ingram,Danielle N. Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个围绕虚构组织Nebulon构建的合成企业钓鱼邮件数据集，包含匹配的合法与钓鱼邮件及钓鱼线索注释，并通过人类分类和分类器评估验证了其在钓鱼检测和人类易感性研究中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钓鱼邮件是最持久的网络安全威胁之一，但公开可共享的、针对真实企业邮件环境研究钓鱼攻击的数据集仍然有限，这阻碍了该领域的进一步研究。

Method: 基于虚构组织Nebulon构建合成企业钓鱼邮件数据集，覆盖广泛的工作场所沟通主题，包含匹配的合成合法邮件和钓鱼邮件，并提供可解释的钓鱼线索注释。

Result: 人类受试者分类和分类器评估表明，该数据集在支持有意义的钓鱼判断变异性的同时，也为监督式检测提供了可学习信号。

Conclusion: 该公开资源旨在支持未来在钓鱼检测、人类易感性、可解释性和类企业场景基准开发方面的研究工作。

Abstract: Phishing remains one of the most persistent cyber threats, yet publicly shareable datasets for studying phishing in realistic enterprise email settings remain limited. To address this gap, we introduce a synthetic enterprise phishing email dataset built around a fictitious organization, Nebulon. The dataset spans a broad set of workplace communication themes and includes matched synthetic legitimate and phishing emails with interpretable phishing-cue annotations. Here, ``legitimate'' denotes the non-phishing class, not legitimately occurring organizational emails. Human-subject categorizations and classifier evaluations show that the dataset supports meaningful variation in phishing judgments while also providing learnable signal for supervised detection. This publicly released resource is intended to support future work on phishing detection, human susceptibility, explainability, and benchmark development in enterprise-like contexts.

</details>


### [156] [Client-Side Probing of Deleted Ridge Statistics in Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04475)
*Yijun Quan,Giovanni Montana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示联邦遗忘系统中的广播更新可泄露隐藏的客户端数据摘要。恶意客户端通过提交已知更改并分析返回的分类器差异，能在删除操作前后推断出被删除样本、类别或客户端的摘要信息，进而实现数据重用攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘系统为了高效实现精确删除，会广播更新后的线性分类器。作者发现这种广播机制存在隐私漏洞——返回的模型状态足以让恶意客户端逆向推断出被删除的数据摘要，这对系统安全构成严重威胁。

Method: 作者在数学上刻画了观测信息何时包含足够的独立信息用于推断，提出了两种攻击估计器：一种适用于无限制探针的最优构造方案，另一种基于攻击者自身数据构成添加的更现实估计器。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，高精度广播使两种探针类型都能对每个被测样本删除实现精确标签恢复；低精度广播显著降低细粒度恢复效果；响应多样性不足时则完全无法识别。

Conclusion: 研究识别出联邦遗忘广播机制中具体的隐私与完整性风险及其代数成因，并指出广播精度、更新验证、响应速率和并发活动等实际限制因素对攻击效果的影响边界。

Abstract: Federated unlearning aims to remove a client's data from a shared model without retraining from scratch. Some efficient systems make deletion exact by storing compact, additive summaries of the training features and broadcasting an updated linear classifier after every accepted change. We show that these broadcasts can also reveal the hidden summaries. A malicious client can submit known changes, use the returned classifiers to identify the server state, and compare states immediately before and after an isolated deletion. This exposes the deleted sample, class, or client summary and can enable its reinsertion. We characterize exactly when the observations contain enough independent information, give a matching optimal construction for unrestricted probes, and derive a more realistic estimator based on additions formed from the attacker's own data. On MNIST and CIFAR-10, high-precision broadcasts permit exact label recovery for every tested sample deletion with both probe types. Lower-precision broadcasts sharply reduce fine-grained recovery, and insufficiently diverse responses prevent identification altogether. Unrestricted probes are readily detected by their size; most individual attacker-data additions resemble honest batches, although we do not claim that the complete sequence is inconspicuous. The results identify a concrete privacy and integrity risk, its algebraic cause, and practical limits involving broadcast precision, update verification, response rate, and concurrent activity.

</details>


### [157] [Hoss: Fast Oblivious Semantic Search with Heterogeneous GPU-CPU-TEE Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04522)
*Jianzhang Du,Weijie Huang,Chenghong Wang,Nicolas Tsagareli,Yukui Luo,XiaoFeng Wang,Zhongshu Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Hoss 是一个基于异构 CPU-GPU TEE 架构的 oblivious 语义搜索系统，利用 GPU TEE 的大容量私有内存（Pmem）来消除传统 ORAM 的开销瓶颈，在保持高召回率的同时实现了相较于现有最优系统 Compass 最高 67 倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 oblivious 语义搜索系统 Compass 虽然已通过优化 ORAM 移除了大部分密码学开销，但 ORAM 访问仍受制于 Omega(log N) 带宽下界，性能差距难以弥合。作者观察到传统 ORAM 开销源于私有内存受限的假设，而现代 GPU TEE 提供了大容量私有内存，可以掩盖内部访问模式，这开辟了新的设计空间。

Method: Hoss 采用异构 CPU-GPU TEE 架构：GPU TEE 的大容量私有内存承载热路径 HNSW 图遍历，超出 GPU 容量的图低层则卸载到 CPU TEE，仅在访问这些低层时才调用 oblivious 原语。此外，大容量私有内存支持了 host-access ORAM 机制，突破了传统性能约束，并引入多种依赖数据的优化。

Result: 原型系统测试表明，Hoss 相较于 Compass 实现了最高 67 倍的加速，同时保持高召回率，且规模越大加速比越显著。

Conclusion: 利用现代 GPU TEE 的大容量私有内存特性，可以有效突破传统 ORAM 的带宽下界限制，为 oblivious 语义搜索系统提供了一种高效、可扩展且低成本的新架构方案。

Abstract: Semantic search is widely deployed in modern AI systems, but protecting both data contents and access patterns remains challenging. The current state-of-the-art system, Compass, achieves oblivious semantic search by building an optimized ORAM over HNSW graphs. However, even with aggressive optimizations, it still incurs large overheads. Closing this performance gap is fundamentally difficult: Compass has already removed most cryptographic overheads, leaving ORAM accesses as the dominant cost, which are constrained by well-known Omega(log N) bandwidth lower bounds. Our key insight is that traditional ORAM overhead stems from the assumption of limited private memory, whereas modern GPU TEEs provide large private memory (Pmem) that blinds internal access patterns (Hunt et al., NSDI '23). This shift opens a new design space. We therefore propose Hoss, a first-of-its-kind oblivious semantic search system with a heterogeneous CPU-GPU TEE architecture that supports fast, scalable search with low cost of ownership. In Hoss, the GPU TEE's large Pmem hosts the hot-path HNSW traversal, while the lower layers of the graph, if they exceed GPU capacity, are offloaded to CPU TEEs. The system invokes oblivious primitives only when accessing these lower layers. The availability of large Pmem also enables new optimization opportunities. For example, Hoss features a host-access ORAM mechanism that goes beyond traditional performance constraints and incorporates several data-dependent optimizations that are not possible in prior designs. We implement a prototype of Hoss and benchmark it against Compass. Our results show that Hoss achieves up to 67x speedup while maintaining high recall, with larger gains at scale.

</details>


### [158] [Repeat-After-Me: Black-Box Adaptive Visual Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04533)
*Sizhe Chen,Yu-Lin Tsai,Ivan Evtimov,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner,Arman Zharmagambetov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Repeat-After-Me，一种黑盒自适应视觉提示注入攻击方法，可在商业级VLM（如Qwen3.6-27B和GPT-5.5）上实现超过80%和47%的攻击成功率，能泄露个人隐私信息或发起恶意工具调用，并在真实OpenClaw agent部署中验证了其实战威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本域的黑盒提示注入已能达到近乎完美的攻击成功率，但现有视觉提示注入方法在攻击商业级VLM时效果有限，特别是在需要生成长且格式严格的目标字符串（如精确的可解析原生工具调用）时难度很大。这促使作者探索更有效的视觉提示注入攻击方法。

Method: 提出Repeat-After-Me攻击方法，属于黑盒自适应视觉提示注入；在用户提示与注入任务语义无关且无口头授权的情况下进行攻击；利用图像作为注入载体，在替身模型上优化后迁移到目标商业VLM；在真实OpenClaw agent（Discord部署）中验证攻击向量，通过最小注入图像覆盖TOOLS.md实现远程代码执行和敏感数据窃取。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-27B和GPT-5.5上分别实现超过80%和47%的攻击成功率；跨模型迁移保留了43-46%的攻击成功率，跨样本迁移保留了64-66%；在真实OpenClaw agent中成功实现恶意行为（覆盖TOOLS.md、远程代码执行和秘密数据窃取）；该攻击向量在自适应文本提示注入失败的情况下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 视觉提示注入对商业级VLM构成了严重的现实安全威胁，Repeat-After-Me展示了视觉攻击向量可以突破文本提示注入失效的场景，凸显了多模态AI agent安全防护的紧迫性，文中讨论了潜在的防御策略。

Abstract: Prompt injection is widely recognized as a major security threat to AI agents that interact with untrusted external data, such as websites, documents, and emails. Prior work has shown that, in the text domain, black-box prompt injection can achieve near-perfect attack success rates (ASRs). In the image domain, however, existing visual prompt injection methods are substantially less effective in attacking frontier commercial VLMs for materially harmful behavior. Achieving such outputs is hard because it requires a long and/or format-compliant target string, such as a precise, parseable native tool call with exact function names and arguments. We present Repeat-After-Me, a black-box adaptive visual prompt injection attack that can reveal personally identifiable information or make malicious tool calls. Across both open-weight and commercial frontier VLMs, including Qwen3.6-27B and GPT-5.5, our method achieves ASRs exceeding 80% and 47%, respectively, under a realistic setting in which the benign user prompt is semantically unrelated to the injected task and does not verbally authorize it. In our evaluation, injections optimized on one surrogate retain 43-46% of the original ASR on two commercial victims, and cross-sample transferability retains 64-66% of the original ASR on those two models. We test our attack in a real-world OpenClaw agent: in a default OpenClaw Discord deployment, an untrusted user can use a minimally injected image to overwrite TOOLS.md, enabling future sensitive behaviors like remote code execution and secret exfiltration. We show our new attack vector works in cases where adaptive textual prompt injection fails. We discuss potential defenses.

</details>


### [159] [Optimizing Credential Blast Radius Through Trust Boundaries and Delegation Under Post-Quantum Authentication Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04566)
*Pauli Taipale,Harri Lainio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合优化信任域划分与凭据派生结构的框架，在策略与延迟约束下，通过加权最小割等方法最小化凭据爆炸半径（服务影响），并证明该问题在一般情况下为NP难，但两域直接签发场景可简化为加权最小割问题。实验表明联合优化比先划分边界再选择凭据结构的方法在230个穷举合成比较中的195个上表现更优，在延迟预算内相比单域设计可降低最多36%的期望影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将交互服务划分到独立信任域可以限制签发者被攻破后的影响范围，但会增加跨信任边界的调用次数；后量子公钥认证与密钥建立机制的替换会进一步增加受限或丢包路径上的跨域延迟。现有的信任域划分与凭据派生结构选择通常是分离进行的，缺乏在策略和延迟约束下联合优化的系统性方法。作者希望将这两个相互关联的设计决策联合求解，从而在满足延迟预算的前提下最小化某方被攻破后的凭据波及范围。

Method: 本文形式化了联合选择信任域与凭据派生结构的问题，通过域分配将服务交互图与凭据派生图关联起来。凭据爆炸半径以加权服务影响度量攻破后的影响；使用线性上界支持优化，并建立联合事件模型给出精确期望影响。对共享签发者场景，在凭据可达区域不重叠时上界精确，否则需显式传播。作者证明一般问题为NP难，但标量化的两域直接签发可归约为加权最小割问题。实验采用轨迹回放方法，结合实测后量子成本、合成风险输入、固定派生族及1至6个信任域进行验证。

Result: 在230个穷举合成比较中，联合优化在195个场景中比"先选边界再选结构"的两阶段方法获得了更低的爆炸半径，尤其在链式委派场景下优势明显。使用实测后量子成本与合成风险输入的轨迹回放实验显示，在延迟预算内，最优设计相对单域方案可将期望影响降低最多36%。

Conclusion: 该框架将风险假设与实测跨域成本转化为候选信任域与凭据派生设计，表明信任域与凭据结构应当作为整体进行联合优化，而不是分步独立决策。两域直接签发场景下的加权最小割简化使得求解可行，为实际系统中的后量子迁移设计提供了可操作的优化方法。

Abstract: Partitioning interacting services into independently rooted trust domains limits issuer-compromise reach while increasing calls across trust boundaries. Post-quantum replacements for public-key authentication and key-establishment mechanisms can increase crossing latency on constrained or lossy paths. We formulate the joint selection of trust domains and credential-derivation structures under policy and latency constraints, linking separate service-interaction and credential-derivation graphs through domain assignment. Credential blast radius measures weighted service impact after compromise. A linear upper bound supports optimization, while a joint event model gives exact expected impact. For shared issuers, the bound is exact under nonoverlapping credential reach and otherwise requires explicit propagation. While the general problem is NP-hard, scalarized two-domain direct issuance reduces to a weighted minimum cut. Joint optimization yields lower blast radius than choosing boundaries first in 195 of 230 exhaustive synthetic comparisons, especially under chained delegation. A trace-derived replay used measured post-quantum costs, synthetic risk inputs, a fixed derivation family, and one to six trust domains. The best design found reduced expected impact by up to 36% relative to one domain within the latency budget. The framework turns risk assumptions and measured crossing costs into candidate trust-domain and credential-derivation designs.

</details>


### [160] [Why Is SHAP Not a Reliable Standalone Explanation Framework for Malware Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04626)
*Seyedreza Mohseni,Edward Raff,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证SHAP对恶意软件检测模型的可解释性保证不足。在静态PE文件特征空间中，由于特征之间存在结构性依赖，SHAP的解释结果会严重失真：条件SHAP会稀释特征贡献、归因于模型未使用的特征，甚至反转符号；干预SHAP则查询不存在于真实可执行文件中的离流形组合。实验在EMBER和BODMAS数据集上证实了这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习广泛用于恶意软件检测，但其决策需要可解释性。分析师需要确认模型是否学到了真实的恶意行为而非仅数据集特有的模式。目前SHAP被视为标准解释工具，但其正式保证是否足以支撑恶意软件解释尚存疑问。

Method: 通过理论分析，文章证明在静态PE特征空间中特征间的结构性依赖（如字节直方图、熵、导入表等）会导致条件SHAP产生三种缺陷：稀释特征信用至1/m、将重要性归因于模型未使用的特征、在数据分布变化时反转未使用特征的归因符号。同时指出干预SHAP会查询离流形联盟。文章在EMBER-2018、EMBER-2024和BODMAS数据集上用固定LightGBM和XGBoost检测器进行了实验验证。

Result: 理论证明与实验结果均确认：在PE恶意软件检测场景中，SHAP的解释不可靠。条件SHAP因特征依赖而产生稀释、误归因和符号反转；干预SHAP则生成不真实的离流形假设组合。SHAP的实际解释严重依赖于分析师对特征选择、缺失规则、背景分布和简化输入映射的预先设定。

Conclusion: SHAP应被定位为有限的诊断工具，需结合明确声明的数据分布和领域验证使用，而非对恶意软件行为的独立完整解释。其形式化保证不足以支撑恶意软件检测场景中的可靠解释，需要开发者在使用时审视SHAP内部游戏的设定前提。

Abstract: Machine learning is widely used for malware detection, but its decisions must be explained. An analyst needs to know whether a model has learned genuine malicious behavior or only dataset-specific patterns \cite{gaur2021semantics}. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) is the standard tool for this, backed by formal properties such as local accuracy, missingness, and consistency. We argue that these guarantees are insufficient for reliable malware interpretation. We claim SHAP explains a chosen feature-coalition game, not malware behavior in the data. That game is fixed only after the analyst selects the feature players, the missing feature rule, the background distribution, and the simplified input mapping. In static Portable Executable feature spaces, groups such as byte histograms, byte-entropy, strings, headers, sections, imports, and data-directories are not independent signals but are jointly shaped by file structure, packing, compiler behavior, and family conventions. We prove that this dependence makes conditional SHAP dilute a model's feature credit by a factor of $1/m$ across $m-1$ redundant features, attributes importance to features the model never uses, and even reverses the sign of an unused feature's attribution when the data distribution changes; interventional SHAP, meanwhile, queries off-manifold coalitions that no real executable would exhibit. Experiments on EMBER-2018, EMBER-2024, and BODMAS with fixed LightGBM and XGBoost detectors confirm these effects. We therefore position SHAP as a limited diagnostic that requires an explicitly stated data distribution and domain validation, not a standalone account of malware behavior.

</details>


### [161] [Memory-Efficient Designs for Word-Wise Universal Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04769)
*Ardhi Wiratama Baskara Yudha,Erwin Eko Wahyudi,Rian Adam Rajagede,Qian Lou,Yan Solihin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BXT是一个针对全同态加密（FHE）的优化框架，通过四种技术（密文压缩、密文序列化、延迟种子生成和基于容错训练的密文数字剪枝）缓解FHE执行中的内存瓶颈，在CNN推理中实现了最高3.8倍加速，同时精度损失小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE虽然隐私保护能力极强，但执行速度远慢于原始计算。随着计算加速取得进展，性能瓶颈已转移至内存——FHE将数据规模放大数个数量级，导致算术强度低，内存瓶颈成为主要制约因素。

Method: BXT框架提出四种优化技术：(1)密文压缩——执行过程中从种子重新生成密文分量；(2)密文序列化——将系数打包为位数组并在L2到L1传输时解包；(3)延迟种子生成——将PRNG密集的离线工作延迟到聚合操作中执行；(4)针对Universal FHE的密文数字剪枝，由容错感知训练指导。

Result: 在CNN推理上，BXT-CSO50配置相对于100x GPU基线实现了最高3.8倍加速，在50%比较精度下精度损失小于1%。

Conclusion: BXT通过系统性地应对内存瓶颈，有效提升了FHE的执行效率，证明了在保持隐私和精度的前提下，通过内存优化可以显著缩小FHE与原始计算之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data, preserving privacy throughout analysis. While its privacy is very strong, FHE is much slower to execute than the original computation. In particular, due to the recent success in accelerating its compute, the performance bottleneck shifts to the memory, especially considering that FHE magnifies the data size by orders of magnitude, resulting in a low arithmetic intensity.
  We propose BXT, an FHE optimization framework that mitigates the memory bottleneck through four techniques: (1) ciphertext compression, which regenerates ciphertext components from seeds during execution; (2) ciphertext serialization, which packs coefficients as bit arrays and unpacks them during L2-to-L1 transfer; (3) delayed seed generation, which defers PRNG-heavy offline work across aggregated operations; and (4) ciphertext digit pruning guided by fault-aware training tailored for Universal FHE. On CNN inference, the BXT-CSO50 configuration effectively achieves up to 3.8$\times$ speedup over the 100x GPU baseline with less than 1% accuracy loss at 50% comparison precision.

</details>


### [162] [Injected and Leaked: Actively Inducing Side-Channel Leakage Using Electromagnetic Injection and Hardware Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04785)
*Haoran Yan,Ziyu Shao,Shuhao Zhang,Qinhong Jiang,Yan Long*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"注入诱导电磁侧信道"的新型攻击框架，通过电磁注入将低频秘密信号上变频到可测量的电磁辐射频段，实现了远距离被动窃听，突破了传统电磁侧信道分析中信号过弱不可行的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的电磁侧信道泄漏与电磁注入通常被视为两类独立的物理现象，分别威胁数据的机密性和完整性。然而，许多低频秘密信号（如音频、传感器数据）由于频率过低或幅度过弱，难以通过传统电磁侧信道方法直接窃听。作者希望探索电磁注入如何主动放大原本不可行的侧信道泄漏，从而建立一个统一的、闭环的电磁安全分析框架。

Method: 作者提出了一个集成化的闭环电磁安全分析框架，包括：(1) 理论建模，揭示非线性硬件组件（如放大器、模数转换器和功率转换器）能将秘密电信号调制到注入的电磁载波上，实现低频秘密的上变频；(2) 通过调节注入频率和幅度，主动塑造泄漏信号的频谱和熵；(3) 设计了名为InjectEave的攻击方法，并进行了多场景实验验证，包括有线和无线耳机音频窃听、穿墙场景、智能家居设备功耗窃听及模拟传感器输入窃听；(4) 通过案例研究展示了固话对话的闭环窃听和操控能力。

Result: 实验表明，InjectEave攻击能够在30米距离外利用商用射频设备窃听有线和无线耳机的音频播放，并能在穿墙场景中实现窃听。此外还成功表征了智能家居设备功耗和模拟传感器输入等低频秘密的注入诱导电磁泄漏，并演示了对固话对话的闭环窃听与操控。

Conclusion: 电磁注入可以将原本不可行的低频侧信道泄漏放大到可被远程探测的程度，攻击者能够主动塑造泄漏信号的特征，使得侧信道攻击从被动监听升级为主动操控。论文分析了这一新型安全威胁的挑战，并讨论了相应的缓解措施。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel leakage and injection are typically treated as distinct physical phenomena, threatening data confidentiality and integrity respectively. This work investigates how EM injection can be used to amplify side-channel leakage that is otherwise infeasible. We introduce a novel framework for Injection-Induced EM Side Channels to enable integrated, closed-loop EM security analysis. Our theoretical modeling and experimental measurements reveal that nonlinear hardware components, such as ubiquitous amplifiers, analog-to-digital converters, and power converters, can modulate secret electrical signals onto an injected EM carrier and thus upconvert low-frequency secrets into measurable EM emissions. By tuning the injection frequency and amplitude, adversaries gain the ability to actively shape the effective spectrum and entropy of the resulting leakage. We design InjectEave attack and demonstrate eavesdropping on the audio played through wired and wireless headphones from up to 30 m away with accessible RF equipment, as well as in through-wall scenarios, and characterize injection-induced EM leakage of other low-frequency secrets such as power consumption of smart home devices and analog sensor inputs. Case studies further demonstrate how the proposed techniques enable closed-loop eavesdropping and manipulation of landline-phone conversations. Finally, we analyze the broader security challenges and mitigations.

</details>


### [163] [Cost-Aware Hierarchical Multi-Agent Ransomware Detection and Family Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04820)
*Mubashar Iqbal,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种成本感知的分层多智能体系统（HMAS）用于自适应勒索软件检测。该系统以静态分析作为低成本初始模态，仅在置信度不足或专家分歧时选择性启用动态和内存模态，由元编排器协调各领域控制器。通过成本模型平衡分析性能与计算开销，并在困难案例中引入本地部署的大语言模型进行验证。实验表明该架构在保持高精度的同时大幅降低了计算成本和分析延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件检测与家族归属需要分析多种模态（静态、动态、内存等），因为恶意软件可能采用加壳、混淆、进程操纵和运行时规避等手段。然而传统多模态方法对每个样本都使用全部模态，导致不必要的计算成本和延迟增加，亟需一种自适应、成本感知的检测框架。

Method: 提出成本感知的分层多智能体系统（HMAS），采用元编排器协调分层领域控制器的架构。静态分析作为初始低成本模态，当置信度不足或专家智能体间存在分歧时，选择性添加动态和内存模态。构建包含模态使用与处理开销的成本模型来引导编排策略，在分析性能与计算成本间取得平衡。同时，本地部署的大语言模型仅用于校验困难案例，不替代确定性检测管线。

Result: 完整HMAS在二进制勒索软件检测中达到96.57%准确率、0.96 F1分数和0.99 ROC-AUC；在家族归属任务中达到0.90宏F1分数。相比穷举分析方法，平均分析成本降低43.97%，平均延迟大幅降低（LLM介入场景除外）。路由分析显示56.05%的案例仅凭静态证据即可解决，仅4.33%需要完整证据管线。

Conclusion: 自适应HMAS能够为勒索软件分析提供准确率与成本之间的有效权衡，同时支持异构和不完整模态。该架构证明了在保持高检测性能的前提下，通过智能模态选择可显著降低计算开销的可行性。

Abstract: Ransomware detection and family attribution require analysis of different modalities because it can use packing, obfuscation, process manipulation and runtime evasion techniques. However, conventional multimodal usually uses all available modalities for every sample resulting in unnecessary computational cost and increased latency. In this paper, we present a Cost Aware Hierarchical Multi-Agent System (HMAS) for adaptive ransomware detection. The proposed architecture organizes specialized agents into hierarchical domain controllers coordinated by a Meta Orchestrator. Static analysis is used as the initial low-cost modality while additional dynamic and memory modality is selectively used when confidence is insufficient or specialist agents exhibit disagreement. A cost model incorporates modality use and processing overhead. It enables the orchestration policy to balance analysis performance against computational cost. A locally deployed large language model provides verification for selected difficult cases without replacing the deterministic pipeline. Experimental evaluation compares adaptive HMAS with static only, static plus dynamic and exhaustive analysis policies across binary ransomware detection and multiclass family attribution. The complete HMAS achieved 96.57% accuracy, 0.96 F1-score and 0.99 ROC-AUC for binary detection. It also achieved 0.90 macro-F1 for family attribution. At the same time, the HMAS reduced average analysis cost by 43.97% relative to exhaustive analysis and substantially reduced average analysis latency except for the case where LLM is used. Routing analysis showed that 56.05% of cases were resolved using static evidence alone. Only 4.33% required the complete evidence pipeline. These findings demonstrate that adaptive HMAS can provide accuracy cost tradeoff for ransomware analysis while retaining support for heterogeneous and incomplete modalities.

</details>


### [164] [Forgetting Without Restarting: Execution-State Unlearning for Stateful LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04875)
*Chao Yao,Yangbo Wei,Zhen Huang,Junhong Qian,Chenle Chen,Shaoqiang Lu,Chen Wu,Lei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了LLM智能体的"执行态遗忘"问题，提出基于溯源的选择性重放方法，通过跨层溯源图定位注入点、裁剪KV缓存恢复检查点、并重放消除污染的后续轨迹，实现精确的类计数遗忘（T-τ+1次重计算）。实验证明其在多个基准上优于记忆删除和基于指令的遗忘方法，效果与完全重置相当且重算令牌量减少最多9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的LLM智能体是有状态的，积累了压缩摘要、明文记忆、工具计划以及KV缓存等多种衍生工件。现有"遗忘"操作仅删除明文记忆记录就停止，所有从被撤销信息派生的工件仍然完好，导致被撤销信息实际上无法彻底消除。

Method: 将运行时建模为确定性转移系统，形式化证明精确遗忘所需的重计算次数下界；提出Provenance-Guided Selective Replay（溯源引导的选择性重放），通过跨层溯源图定位目标注入点，将检查点恢复简化为KV缓存裁剪，再利用净化后的重放重建反事实后缀，形成覆盖提示词、压缩记忆和缓存的跨层契约。

Result: 在三套智能体套件、九个基线和三个模型家族上，使用elicitation、随机和免字符串行为测试审计：记忆删除后泄露水平不变，基于指令的遗忘在诱发测试下完全失效（Leak@probes=1.00），源文本删改仍会在80%的回合中作用于被撤销偏好；而选择性重放在最多减少9倍重计算令牌的情况下与完全重置不可区分。

Conclusion: 精确的智能体遗忘不能只靠删除明文记忆，必须考虑所有派生工件；溯源引导的选择性重放提供了一种理论最优且可验证的有效方案，实现了与完全重置等价的遗忘效果同时显著节省计算资源。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents are stateful: beyond the transcript they accrete compressed summaries, plaintext memory, pending tool plans, and, under every serving API, a KV cache. Yet today's "forget" operations delete a plaintext memory record and stop, leaving every artifact derived from the revoked information intact. We formalize execution-state unlearning: after a forget request, the agent must behave as if it had never observed the target. Modeling the runtime as a deterministic transition system, we prove that the pre-target trajectory prefix is shared with this counterfactual world for free, that the post-target suffix is irreducibly tainted without token-level attribution, and that exact unlearning requires at least $T-τ+1$ recomputed transitions, where $τ$ is the target's injection step. Provenance-Guided Selective Replay attains this bound as a cross-layer contract spanning prompt, compressed memory, and cache: a provenance graph locates the injection point, checkpoint restoration reduces to cropping the KV cache, and sanitized replay regenerates the counterfactual suffix. Audited with elicitation, stochastic, and string-free behavioral tests across three agent suites, nine baselines, and three model families, memory deletion leaves leakage unchanged, instruction-based forgetting collapses under elicitation (Leak@probes = 1.00), and source redaction still acts on a revoked preference in 80% of episodes, while selective replay is indistinguishable from a full reset at up to 9x fewer recomputed tokens.

</details>


### [165] [ReCAST: Restoration-aware Cascaded Stage-wise Training for Obfuscated SMS Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04878)
*Jieyun Huang,Yi Shen,Kaikai Zhao,Jiangze Yan,Wenjing Zhang,Ping Chen,Ning Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ReCAST框架，通过恢复感知的级联阶段训练，将大模型的去混淆能力蒸馏到小型可部署模型中，实现鲁棒的混淆中文垃圾短信分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文短信中的欺诈消息越来越多地采用精心设计的混淆策略来规避基于成本的分类器，这些混淆在保持人类可读性的同时隐藏风险短语，导致直接分类在生产环境的延迟和吞吐约束下表现脆弱。

Method: ReCAST采用三阶段监督：混淆跨度检测、混淆类型预测和文本恢复，将大型教师模型的去混淆能力蒸馏到小型学生模型中，再利用恢复感知的学生模型进行下游风险分类。

Result: 在内部构建的真实中文短信基准测试上，ReCAST在混淆场景下相比直接训练的基线显著提升了分类性能。

Conclusion: 恢复感知的蒸馏为在生产约束下使用更小的可部署模型实现鲁棒短信风险分类提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fraudulent messages sent via Short Message Service (SMS) are increasingly obfuscated to evade cost-conscious classifiers in production systems. In Chinese SMS, attackers can exploit a wide range of carefully crafted obfuscation strategies to hide risk-bearing phrases while preserving human readability, making direct classification brittle under real-world latency and throughput constraints. We propose ReCAST, a Restoration-aware Cascaded Stage-wise Training framework for robust obfuscated Chinese SMS classification. ReCAST distills a large teacher model's de-obfuscation ability into a smaller deployable student model by supervising obfuscated span detection, obfuscation type prediction, and text restoration, and then uses the restoration-aware student for downstream risk classification. Experiments on an internally constructed real-world Chinese SMS benchmark show that ReCAST substantially improves classification performance over directly trained baselines under obfuscation. The results suggest that restoration-aware distillation offers a practical path toward robust SMS risk classification with smaller deployable models under production-oriented constraints.

</details>


### [166] [The Security Feature Location Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04899)
*Kevin Hermann,Sven Peldszus,Thorsten Berger,Adam Shostack*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了"安全特征定位"任务，即在代码中定位与安全功能（如认证、加密）相关的代码位置，帮助开发者理解安全实现并评估系统的安全属性是否得到落实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件安全需要通过认证、加密等安全特性来实现，但开发者往往不清楚系统实际实现了哪些安全功能、这些功能在代码的哪些位置。这阻碍了安全实现的理解和安全属性的验证。

Method: 提出"安全特征定位"（security feature location）这一任务，旨在将代码位置与安全特征关联起来。

Result: 尚在提出任务阶段，该摘要未给出具体的实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过安全特征定位，开发者能够理解安全实现的具体位置，并评估预期中的安全属性是否真正得到执行。

Abstract: Software security must be realized through security features such as authentication and encryption, but which features does a system implement, and where? We present security feature location: the task of relating code locations to security features, enabling developers to understand security implementations and assess whether intended security properties are enforced.

</details>


### [167] [Robust Coverless Linguistic Steganography via Sentence Embedding Space with Global Resynchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04970)
*Lizhi Xiong,Yuping Lu,Jun Li,Ziqiang Li,Zhangjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于句子嵌入空间的鲁棒无载体隐写框架，通过将秘密信息编码为层次聚类路径并结合全局重同步机制，有效应对词级和句子级文本扰动以及比特滑动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐写方法依赖token级操作，在词级和句子级文本扰动下难以保持可靠性；且变长编码方案极易因微小扰动导致比特滑动，造成嵌入与提取比特序列失同步。

Method: 提出在句子嵌入空间而非token空间中操作的鲁棒无载体隐写框架。秘密消息被编码为句子嵌入空间中的层次聚类路径，增强对文本扰动的解码稳定性；引入全局重同步机制（GRM），将变长比特流重构为锚定在语义子空间上的离散符号，将局部嵌入失败与全局消息恢复解耦。

Result: 在词级和句子级扰动下，该方法实现了显著的鲁棒性提升，同时保持了有效的嵌入容量，并表现出对统计分析较强的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过在语义空间中进行编码而非token层面操作，有效解决了现有隐写方法在文本扰动下的鲁棒性不足和比特滑动问题，为鲁棒语言隐写提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Linguistic steganography enables covert communication through natural language. Existing methods heavily rely on token-level operations and struggle to maintain reliability under word- and sentence-level textual perturbations. Moreover, variable-length coding-based schemes are highly susceptible to bit-slippage under minor disturbances, as perturbations cause desynchronization between embedded and extracted bit sequences. To address these issues, we propose a robust coverless steganographic framework that operates in the sentence embedding space rather than the token space. Specifically, secret messages are encoded as hierarchical clustering paths in the sentence embedding space, which enhances decoding stability against word- and sentence-level textual perturbations. To tackle the bit-slippage problem, we introduce a Global Resynchronization Mechanism (GRM) that reframes variable-length bitstreams as discrete symbols anchored to semantic subspaces, decoupling local embedding failures from global message recovery. Experimental results demonstrate that under word- and sentence-level perturbations, our approach achieves substantial improvements in robustness, while maintaining effective embedding capacity and exhibiting strong resistance to statistical analysis.

</details>


### [168] [TPMSpy: Validation of Measured Boot Systems by Low-Level Tracing of TPM Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05011)
*Roman Lacko,Petr Svenda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种平台无关的方法，在虚拟化系统与TPM交互的层面分析低层TPM使用情况，独立重建和验证TPM事件日志，不依赖quote机制。作者在Linux和Windows上验证了该方法，并对2020-2025年间systemd 245-258版本的Linux系统进行了纵向分析，发现测量启动使用模式存在广泛差异，包括未文档化的行为变化和不一致的systemd用户空间服务测量，导致远程证明和LUKS磁盘解密不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Measured Boot机制虽然被现代操作系统广泛部署，但其实际安全性取决于实现是否在预期条件下测量了预期组件，而这一点缺乏系统性检查，现有方法无法独立验证。

Method: 提出平台无关的分析方法，在虚拟化系统-TPM交互层面分析低层TPM使用，不依赖quote机制即可独立重建和验证TPM事件日志，适用于开放和封闭系统，并在Linux和Windows上进行验证。

Result: 对2020-2025年间systemd 245-258版本的Linux系统进行纵向分析，发现测量启动使用模式存在广泛差异，没有统一的用法模式；识别出未文档化的行为变化，发现用户空间systemd服务的测量不一致，导致远程证明和LUKS磁盘解密不可靠。

Conclusion: Measured Boot的实际使用在不同系统间高度分化和不一致，需要定制化分析与验证方法；当前实现中的测量不一致性削弱了远程证明和LUKS解密等依赖可信启动的安全功能。

Abstract: Measured Boot extends trust in a booted system by recording cryptographic measurements of executed software and system state into a Trusted Platform Module (TPM), enabling subsequent verification through remote attestation. Although this mechanism is increasingly deployed in contemporary operating systems, its practical security depends on whether implementations measure the expected components under the expected conditions, yet this is not checked systematically.
  We propose a platform-agnostic method for analysing low-level TPM usage at the level of virtualized system--TPM interactions. It enables independent reconstruction and validation of the TPM Event Log without relying on the quoting mechanism itself. Because it does not depend on implementation details, it is applicable to both open and closed systems. We demonstrate the method on both Linux and Windows and conduct a systematic longitudinal analysis of Linux systems with systemd versions 245--258 (2020--2025), examining how Measured Boot usage evolved and observing wide divergence. No single usage pattern emerged amongst systems, warranting customized analysis.
  The analysis identifies undocumented behavioural changes, reveals inconsistent measurements of user-space systemd services, which prevent reliable remote attestation and LUKS disk decryption on such systems.

</details>


### [169] [Has MIMO decoding been proved hard from lattice problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05013)
*Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Dean和Goldsmith提出的将格问题归约到MIMO解码的多项式时间归约进行了理论检验，发现该归约的已发表证明未能成功建立所声称的MIMO解码硬度，并区分了硬度证明中的缺陷与针对特定参数的直接攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMO技术是现代无线通信的基础，物理层安全希望通过利用噪声信道特性来保护信息传输。Dean和Goldsmith提出的归约如果成立，将为物理层安全提供基于成熟格问题难解性的计算基础。然而后续的攻击和反例使其安全性存疑，但归约本身的精确有效性和局限性尚未被完全理解。

Method: 作者对修订后的归约进行了理论检验，识别出LWE归约中未能迁移到非模MIMO场景的结构特征，从而证明已发表的证明无法确立所声称的MIMO解码硬度，并阐明任何修复尝试所需满足的条件。

Result: 结果表明已发表的硬度证明不成立，其缺陷源于LWE归约中的某些结构特征在非模MIMO设置中无法维持。研究同时区分了证明缺陷与针对特定参数选择的直接攻击。

Conclusion: 研究并未排除MIMO系统物理层安全的一般可能性，但证明了所声称的格硬度保证不能从现有归约中推导出来。

Abstract: Multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technology is fundamental to modern wireless communication. Physical layer security seeks to protect transmitted information by exploiting properties of the noisy communication channel. Dean and Goldsmith proposed a polynomial time reduction from lattice problems to MIMO decoding by adapting Regev's reduction for learning with errors (LWE). If valid, this reduction would give physical layer security a strong computational foundation based on the hardness of established lattice problems. Subsequent works presented attacks and counterexamples against the resulting construction, casting doubt on its security but leaving the precise validity and limitations of the underlying reduction incompletely understood. We provide a theoretical examination of the revised reduction and identify the structural features of the LWE reduction that fail to carry over to the non-modular MIMO setting, hence showing that its published proof does not establish the claimed hardness of MIMO decoding. Our results distinguish flaws in the hardness proof from direct attacks on particular parameter choices and clarify what would be required of any attempted repair. We do not rule out physical layer security for MIMO systems in general, but show that the claimed lattice hardness guarantee does not follow from the existing reduction.

</details>


### [170] [Operational Roles of QRNG-Derived Quantum Entropy in Bitcoin Proof-of-Work Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05092)
*Ricardo Fernandes da Silva,Paulo Vitor Batista Santos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过调度层可观测指标η（熵效率因子）和ρ（重启多样性指数）验证了混合量子-经典挖矿基础设施中QRNG熵的操作价值。Monte Carlo与调度器仿真表明，在诚实节点且候选区块头不同的条件下，QRNG替代经典熵不影响PoW成功概率；QRNG的价值主要体现在关联重启故障、命名空间复用和熵来源追溯等保障导向场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在定位和量化量子随机数生成器（QRNG）在混合量子-经典挖矿基础设施中的操作价值，填补现有研究中缺乏可复现基准来评估QRNG熵在实际挖矿系统中作用的空白。

Method: 提出两个调度层可观测指标——熵效率因子η和重启多样性指数ρ，通过Monte Carlo仿真和调度器模拟（含置信区间）对比确定性基线、强经典基线与QRNG场景，并模拟关联重启故障、命名空间复用和熵来源追溯等测试场景。

Result: 在候选区块头保持不同的诚实PoW场景下，QRNG与经典熵无显著差异；QRNG的操作价值在涉及关联重启故障、命名空间复用和熵来源追溯的保障导向场景中显现；仿真结果具有置信区间支撑。

Conclusion: 本文定位为基于仿真的验证框架而非设备级QRNG演示，下一步实验方向是引入录制或实时的QRNG数据流进行硬件在环验证。

Abstract: Replacing classical entropy with QRNG output does not change honest Bitcoin PoW success probability when candidate headers remain distinct. The original contribution of this paper is a reproducible benchmark that locates and measures the operational value of quantum entropy in hybrid quantum-classical mining infrastructure through two scheduler-level observables, the entropy-efficiency factor $η$ and the reboot-diversity index $ρ$. Monte Carlo and scheduler simulations with confidence intervals show parity for competent deterministic and strong-classical baselines, while QRNG value emerges in assurance-oriented scenarios involving correlated restart faults, namespace reuse, and entropy provenance. The study is therefore positioned as a simulation-based validation framework rather than as a device-level QRNG demonstration; hardware-in-the-loop validation with recorded or live QRNG streams is identified as the next experimental step.

</details>


### [171] [TIER: Threat Implicitness Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Safety Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05117)
*Thu-Hien Trinh-Thi,Hai-Yen Vong,Thanh-Ha Ung-Dung,Tram Ho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TIER（威胁隐含性基准），用于评估LLM在不同威胁隐含程度下的行为安全表现，涵盖四个风险域和四个威胁等级，使用六标签行为量表和双LLM法官进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准大多依赖二元指标（如拒绝或接受），忽略了模型在面对不同威胁隐含程度的有害提示时如何响应的问题，缺乏对模型安全行为的细粒度理解。

Method: 构建TIER基准，覆盖四个风险域和四个威胁等级（从显式有害请求到复杂越狱攻击），采用六标签行为量表评估响应，并使用两个独立的LLM法官进行评判。在六个开源LLM上进行实验。

Result: 实验发现：1）模型的安全行为随威胁等级逐渐演变，而非从拒绝直接跳转至顺从；2）语境化提示产生最多样化的行为；3）越狱攻击暴露最大的鲁棒性差距；4）具有相似攻击成功率的模型可能表现出不同的响应分布。

Conclusion: 二元安全指标不足以全面评估LLM安全性，需要引入行为感知的细粒度安全评估方法，以揭示模型在不同威胁隐含程度下的真实安全表现差异。

Abstract: Current LLM safety benchmarks largely rely on binary metrics, overlooking how models respond to harmful prompts with varying threat implicitness. We introduce TIER, a Threat Implicitness Benchmark for behavioral safety evaluation of LLMs. TIER covers four risk domains and four threat levels, from explicit harmful requests to sophisticated jailbreaks. Responses are assessed using a six-label behavior scale and two independent LLM judges. Experiments on six open-weight LLMs show that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels rather than shifting directly from refusal to compliance. Contextual prompts yield the most diverse behaviors, while jailbreaks reveal the largest robustness gaps. Furthermore, models with similar Attack Success Rates can exhibit distinct response distributions, highlighting the need for behavior-aware LLM safety evaluation.

</details>


### [172] [Understanding the Privacy-Preserving Potential of HTTP/2 Against Webpage Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05119)
*Bogdan Cebere,Prateek Kumar,Sylvain Chatel,Wouter Lueks,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本论文研究了HTTP/2应用层的网站指纹（WF）防御方法。作者展示了如何利用HTTP/2特性在客户端和服务端模拟已知防御（如HTTPOS、LLaMA、FRONT、Tamaraw、ALPaCA），并利用HTTP/2的推送、多路复用和流量控制等特性开发新型轻量级防御。通过统一评估框架（包含参数校准、攻击模型、信息论泄漏估计和开销测量）验证了这些防御的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的WF防御大多在Tor或VPN等封装协议层面研究，很少关注HTTP/2应用层的防御能力。然而HTTP/2是最广泛部署的HTTP版本，其特性为轻量级WF防御提供了未开发潜力，值得深入研究。

Method: 作者在HTTP/2客户端模拟了HTTPOS、LLaMA、FRONT、Tamaraw等已知防御，在服务端模拟ALPaCA和Tamaraw。同时利用HTTP/2的主动资源推送、多路复用和流量控制等特性设计了新型防御。评估使用统一的框架：按数据集校准防御参数，组合实用攻击测试、信息论泄漏估计和开销测量，并针对每个防御找到最强的超参数调优指纹识别模型，用两种信息论泄漏估计器评估残余不确定性。

Result: 论文展示了通过HTTP/2特性在客户端和服务端均可有效模拟既有WF防御，并证明HTTP/2特有的功能（推送建议、多路复用、流控制）可以作为轻量但有效的防御手段部署在通信两端。统一评估框架成功识别了各防御的最强攻击模型及防御带来的信息论残余不确定性。

Conclusion: HTTPS/2应用层特性能够支持多种WF防御策略，并且具有部署在客户端和服务端两侧的可行性。论文提出的统一评估框架为公平比较不同防御方法的隐私-开销权衡提供了系统性工具，揭示了HTTP/2在抗WF攻击方面值得进一步探索的潜力。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer which webpage a user visits from encrypted HTTPS traffic alone, compromising privacy even without decryption. WF defenses commonly shape traffic through noise, padding, delays, or flow splitting, yet they are most often studied from the perspective of encapsulating protocols like Tor or VPN rather than at the application layer (HTTP).
  In this work, we focus on application-layer defenses enabled by the most widely deployed version of HTTP, HTTP/2. We demonstrate how known defenses can be emulated through HTTP/2 features at the client side (HTTPOS, LLaMA, FRONT, Tamaraw) and the server side (ALPaCA, Tamaraw). We further show that HTTP/2 features, such as proactive resource suggestion, multiplexing, and flow control, offer untapped potential for lightweight yet effective defenses deployable at both endpoints.
  We evaluate these defenses using a unified blueprint that calibrates defense parameters per dataset, then combines practical attacks, information-theoretic leakage estimates, and overhead measurements. For each defense, this framework identifies the strongest hyperparameter-tuned fingerprinting model and estimates the residual uncertainty induced by the defense using two information-theoretic leakage estimators, all while accounting for the defense's privacy-overhead trade-offs.

</details>


### [173] [Conformal Prediction for Offensive Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05165)
*Giovanni Cherubin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了共形预测（CP）在网络安全领域，特别是攻击性安全（offensive security）中的应用缺口。尽管CP已被广泛用于防御性安全，但其在隐私保护机器学习和网络流量分析这两个关键攻击性安全领域的应用鲜有研究。作者基于上述两个领域展示了初步研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测（CP）自提出已超过25年，但在网络安全领域的应用非常有限。作者注意到CP已被用作防御手段出现在许多近期研究中，但其在攻击性安全（即用于实施攻击）方面的应用在文献中几乎无法追溯。这一明显的应用缺口激发了作者探索CP在攻击性安全中的潜力。

Method: 作者聚焦于两个关键的攻击性安全领域进行探索性研究：（1）隐私保护机器学习——利用CP的特性来辅助或增强攻击场景中的信息推断；（2）网络流量分析——探索CP在利用网络流量数据进行攻击行为或活动分析中的应用可能性。作者通过初步实验在这两个方向上展示CP在攻击性场景中的可行性和价值。

Result: 初步研究结果表明，CP在攻击性安全领域存在尚未开发的巨大潜力，特别是在隐私保护机器学习的攻击场景以及网络流量分析中。文章展示了这些领域中CP应用的可能性，填补了文献中CP在进攻性安全应用的空缺。

Conclusion: CP在安全性攻击方向的应用仍是一个有待深入探索的研究领域。本文通过两个关键方向的初步研究，呼吁学界关注CP在攻击性安全中的价值，暗示该方向具有广阔的研究前景和应用空间。

Abstract: Despite its introduction more than a quarter century ago, Conformal Prediction (CP) has seen surprisingly few applications to the cyber security world thus far. In particular, we observe that, while CP has been employed as a defensive measure in many recent works, its use for carrying out attacks (i.e., for offensive security) is hard to trace in the literature. We explore this gap, by presenting initial findings in two key areas of offensive security: Privacy-Preserving Machine Learning, and network traffic analysis.

</details>


### [174] [Governing Bring Your Own AI: A Parameterized Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05236)
*Dare Bello,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对员工使用个人生成式AI工具（BYOAI）这一新兴实践，提出了一种参数化治理模型。通过对30篇文献的系统评审，建立了风险分类法和框架参与画像，并开发了五级成熟度阶梯与技术控制架构相耦合的治理模型，用于量化治理成熟度对残余风险的降低程度。研究发现禁止型方案与基线方案的残余风险水平相近，而分层控制型方案能显著降低数据外泄风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 员工越来越多地使用个人拥有的生成式AI工具（如ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude）进行日常工作（即BYOAI）。这是影子AI的一种特殊形式，使用个人账户，游离于企业身份和安全控制之外。现有框架仅针对由组织管理的AI工具设计，无法覆盖使用个人账户的非受管AI工具，存在治理空白。

Method: 通过文献系统评审，建立风险分类法和框架-参与画像；开发参数化治理模型来度量治理成熟度水平对残余风险的降低程度；构建五级成熟度阶梯，并通过控制层覆盖率影响安全结果的链条与技术控制架构相耦合；对30篇文献（24项研究、6份框架文件）进行实证分析。

Result: 研究发现数据暴露和合规是最突出的风险类别，框架参与不一致。模型结果表明，禁止型方案与基线方案产生的残余风险水平相近；在指定参数化条件下，分层控制型方案能显著降低模拟的外泄风险并增加可执行覆盖率。

Conclusion: 研究建立了技术、治理和人为三大相互支撑的安全保障支柱，证明了禁止类策略效果有限，推荐采用分层控制型治理方案应对BYOAI带来的安全风险。

Abstract: Employees are increasingly using personally owned generative AI tools such as ChatGPT, Gemini, and Claude for their daily work. This practice is known as Bring Your Own AI (BYOAI), which is a distinct form of Shadow AI in which employee-authenticated personal accounts are used outside of enterprise identity and security controls. Existing frameworks were designed for AI tools managed by organizations, and their coverage does not extend to unmanaged AI tools used with a personal account. In addressing these issues, we developed a governance model through a systematic review of the literature that produces a risk taxonomy and a framework-engagement profile. We also developed a parameterized governance model that measures how much a level of governance maturity reduces residual risk. A five-level maturity ladder is coupled to a technical control architecture through a chain in which the coverage of the control layer influences the security outcomes. Our study of a curated corpus of 30 records (24 research studies and 6 framework documents) indicated that the most prominent categories identified were data exposure and compliance, and framework engagement was inconsistent.
