Motivation: 软件开发平台上的项目包含大量非代码文档（如bug报告、功能请求、论坛帖子等），这些社会技术文档分散在多个沟通渠道中且以自然语言书写，导致分析困难，存在碎片化和噪声问题。

Method: 提出一种3D可视化方法，将项目社会技术文档中的话题映射为植被覆盖的岛屿，每个文档表示为不同类型的树木；构建了从数据挖掘到交互式渲染的完整可视化原型，利用机器学习技术对文档进行聚类和话题提取。

Result: 通过多个案例研究展示了该方法在揭示项目历史中开发话题讨论情况方面的洞见；此外，开展了N=34的用户研究，进一步验证了该方法在理解社会技术文档及其演化方面的适用性。

Conclusion: 该3D可视化方法能够有效地帮助用户理解项目中的社会技术文档及其演化过程，通过案例研究和用户研究的双重验证，证实了其适用性和实用性。

Abstract: Projects hosted on collaborative software development platforms, such as GitHub, include many non-code artifacts documenting the project's lifecycle, with its challenges, plans, design, and even community. These socio-technical artifacts include, for example, bug reports, feature requests, and forum posts, offering a useful prospect on the project's evolution. However, these artifacts are dispersed over multiple communication channels and written in natural language, making their analysis difficult, as they are fragmented and with considerable noise.
  We present a 3D visualization approach mapping topics found across a project's socio-technical artifacts onto vegetation-covered islands, where the individual artifacts are depicted as trees of various types. The topic islands rise out of the ocean as they become discussed, to sink again when they are no longer so. We built a prototype implementing the entire visualization pipeline, from data mining to interactive rendering, leveraging machine learning techniques to cluster the artifacts and extract their topics. We present, through several case studies, the insights that our approach elicits about discussions of development topics throughout a project's history. The user study we conducted (N=34) further strengthens our conclusions about its suitability for understanding socio-technical artifacts and their evolution.

</details>


### [209] [Ritgard: T(r)opical Islands of Socio-Technical Artifacts on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05278)
*Adam Štěpánek,Marco Raglianti,Jan Byška,Barbora Kozlíková,Michele Lanza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Ritgard工具，它从GitHub仓库中挖掘非代码的社会技术制品（如Issues、Pull Requests和Discussions），将其通过文本嵌入和摘要技术聚合成主题岛屿，并以3D岛屿覆盖树木的可视化方式呈现，岛屿地形随主题活跃度升降，直观展示项目特征和关注点的演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件项目的非代码制品（社会技术制品，STAs）如问题讨论、架构决策等包含了代码无法提供的丰富信息，但这些信息散布于不同沟通渠道且以自然语言书写，难以被轻松提取和探索。

Method: Ritgard工具自动化挖掘GitHub仓库中的Issues、Pull Requests和Discussions等社会技术制品，通过文本嵌入与文本摘要的结合来提取主题，并以3D岛屿上覆盖树木的隐喻进行可视化：每棵树代表一个单个制品，每个岛屿代表一个主题；岛屿地形随主题活跃度变化而升降。

Result: 论文描述了Ritgard工具的使用方式和实现细节，展示了在实现该可视化过程中所面临的众多技术挑战。

Conclusion: Ritgard通过3D岛屿森林的可视化隐喻，将碎片化的社会技术制品整合为可探索的主题视图，有效展示项目特征与关注点在整个生命周期中的演化过程。

Abstract: A software project is more than just code. Non-code artifacts often document the human processes and decisions behind source code. The rationale behind a library change, an architectural decision, a problem encountered by a user are all examples of information typically present in socio-technical artifacts (STAs), created and persisted in channels separate from the repository itself (yet sometimes very close---e.g., GitHub Issues with GitHub repositories). These STAs are a trove of information about the project's architecture and its evolution, containing details and insights that code alone cannot provide. Unfortunately, this information is not easily extracted and explored as STAs are frequently fragmented over different communication channels, and are written in natural language.
  We present Ritgard, a tool that mines GitHub repositories for their STAs, namely Issues, Pull Requests, and Discussions, and visualizes them as 3D islands covered with trees. Each tree represents a single artifact and each island is a topic extracted from the artifacts through a combination of text embedding and text summarization. The terrain of the islands rises out of the ocean as the topic becomes active and sinks back in when it becomes stale, thus depicting the evolution of features and concerns throughout the project's lifetime. We describe the tool's usage and implementation, showing the numerous technical challenges behind Ritgard's visualization.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EXAONE Finance，一个专为金融时间序列预测设计的基础模型。它采用无注意力架构（使用因果一维卷积和分组感知池化MLP替代自注意力），并通过掩码上下文增强提升对缺失数据的鲁棒性。该模型在大规模多资产类别金融语料上预训练，在FinVerse基准上所有三个评估维度均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列基础模型存在三方面局限：自注意力架构的计算成本随序列长度和变量数量呈二次方增长；假设输入完全观测（无法处理金融数据中普遍的缺失问题）；预训练语料无法捕捉金融市场独有的动态特征。这些不足阻碍了它们在金融领域的应用。

Method: 1) 采用无注意力架构，用因果一维卷积做时间混合、用分组感知池化MLP做变量混合，两者均为线性时间复杂度操作；2) 引入掩码上下文增强，在训练中暴露连续的缺失片段，提升对金融市场普遍存在的数据缺失的鲁棒性；3) 在大规模金融语料上预训练，覆盖股票、外汇、大宗商品、加密资产、固定收益及宏观经济指标等多类资产。

Result: 在FinVerse金融预测基准上，EXAONE Finance在所有三个评估层级（点预测精度、横截面资产排序、投资组合盈利能力）均排名第一，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过无注意力架构和针对金融数据特征设计的训练策略，EXAONE Finance有效解决了通用时间序列模型在金融预测中的效率和鲁棒性瓶颈，展示了金融专用TSFM的可行性。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [211] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统化叙述性综述了人工智能招聘从简单的简历配对与排序，演进为多阶段工作流的全过程——包括检索证据、比较候选人及支持或执行行动。通过对40篇代表性工作的分析，识别出三个关键转变（从相似性到互适性、从模型到复合工作流、从离线预测到循证与效率导向评估），并指出了该领域在评估证据方面的关键缺口，如隐私与安全未被联合评估、行为标签混淆暴露偏好与资质等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在招聘中的应用已经从自动化的双档案配对和排名列表，演化为检索证据、比较候选人并支持或执行行动的多阶段工作流。然而现有文献对评估实践与系统演进之间的复杂关系缺乏系统性梳理，特别是从评估证据到可辩护声明的映射不清，因此需要一篇综述来追溯这一发展轨迹并识别持续存在的评估缺口。

Method: 采用目的性检索和编码协议，文献更新截止至2026年7月23日，并通过2026年9月2日的定向更新，共整理40篇代表性工作及配套的工业和法律来源。这不是流行率估计，而是一种分析性综合。对候选人在文档理解、检索、排序、评估、面试、寻源和人工交接等多个环节进行分类，并区分领域级、配对级、列表级、案例级、轨迹级和结果级六种评估证据级别。

Result: 识别出三个相互耦合的转变：从相似性到互适性匹配、从单一模型到复合工作流、从离线预测到循证与效率对齐的评估。发现的持续缺口包括：行为标签混淆了曝光、偏好和资质；私有与合成数据限制了外部效度；最终输出分数掩盖了流程故障；编码集中未直接评估隐私，也没有任何一行同时联合评估效用、公平性、隐私与安全。

Conclusion: 研究引入了一个从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分阶段映射框架，并提出面向互惠、循证、时间受控、选择性和可审计系统的研究议程。进展应以工作流是否检索到正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可争议的决策，并在明确的成本与风险约束下改善结果为标准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [212] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harbor Adapters，一个统一的智能体基准评测基础设施，将超过80个基准适配为可评估任意智能体的形式，并在54个基准上对8个模型进行大规模评测。同时引入Harbor-Index，一个包含82个精选高难度任务（覆盖29个基准）的评测集，最强配置（GPT-5.5 with Codex）通过率仅28.0%，确保评测的挑战性与可负担性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不断增长的智能体基准上评估智能体面临挑战，因为这些基准通常需要复杂的环境和智能体集成，缺乏统一的评测基础设施。

Method: （1）开发基准适配器，将80+基准移植用于评估任意智能体，并通过代码审查和平行实验验证；（2）对8个覆盖不同能力层级的模型在54个基准上进行大规模评估，每个模型使用Terminus-2及3种原生框架之一运行；（3）通过难度过滤、AI与人工审计及审计-修正循环，构建Harbor-Index（82个精选任务，覆盖29个基准）。

Result: 所有模型-框架配置的通过率均未超过30%，最强配置（GPT-5.5 with Codex）达到28.0%。评估结果支持对智能体能力和失败模式进行更广泛的分析。

Conclusion: 发布适配器、评估结果、深入分析及Harbor-Index作为开源资源，以支持更可靠和全面的语言模型智能体评测。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [213] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习（KNN、RF、SVM）结合SMOTE与PCA数据预处理技术，对电力系统N-k故障场景的安全等级进行分类（安全、中等、严重），在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统上验证，RF取得了最优F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统安全对稳定性与可靠性至关重要，传统故障分析方法难以满足实时安全评估需求，需要探索可扩展且高效的机器学习方案来对电力系统故障进行安全等级分类，以支持主动决策并避免大规模停电。

Method: 采用Newton-Raphson潮流方法提取故障场景数据，以总体性能指数（OPI）作为安全度量；使用SMOTE处理类不平衡、PCA降低数据维度；构建四种混合预处理配置（归一化、SMOTE平衡、PCA变换、SMOTE+PCA组合）；训练KNN、RF、SVM三种分类器，并在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统的N-k（k=1,2,3）故障场景数据集上进行评估。

Result: RF在IEEE-30上取得最高F1分数0.97，在IEEE-14上为0.86；SVM显著受益于PCA变换，分类准确率提升明显；KNN最适配于SMOTE+PCA转换。整体上PCA对模型性能的贡献大于SMOTE，SMOTE虽提升召回率但会引入误报，牺牲部分准确率。

Conclusion: 机器学习是传统电力系统故障分析的可扩展且强大的替代方案，能够提升实时安全评估能力。其中RF表现最优，PCA是最具价值的预处理手段，未来可进一步优化SMOTE的使用策略以平衡精确率与召回率。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [214] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Iris-mini和Iris-pro两个搜索智能体（35B-A3B和397B-A17B规模），通过从网页超链接结构中反向构建多跳问答任务，结合SFT-RL交替训练流程，在BrowseComp等基准上取得了开源搜索智能体的最强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体在开放域信息获取中至关重要，但现有方法缺少高质量的训练数据构造方法和系统化的训练流程。作者旨在通过可复现的数据管线与训练配方，提升搜索智能体的多跳推理和工具使用能力。

Method: （1）从网页超链接结构中反向构建多跳任务链，将非答案实体改写为描述性引用以防止字符串匹配，并通过参考模型筛选（闭卷失败但有证据可解）；（2）将问题转化为轨迹后在轨迹级和轮次级过滤，再进行SFT；（3）通过RL对实时搜索进行策略优化，奖励评判器和观测摘要器在训练集群内提供服务，超长回滚在请求级中断并在下一步从已提交前缀恢复；（4）交替SFT和RL两个阶段（称为SFT-RL climbing），将每轮RL中最难解决和最高效的轨迹回传至下一轮监督训练。

Result: 在启用上下文管理的情况下，Iris-mini和Iris-pro在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE上分别达到82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3和88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4的成绩，是各自参数范围内开源搜索智能体中的最强结果。所有结果均来自单一ReAct智能体，不使用子智能体或测试时验证。

Conclusion: 作者计划发布模型权重以及完整的数据构建、训练和评估配方，以推动开源搜索智能体的发展。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [215] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测试时的解释忠实性改进方法，通过从输入中移除解释中未提及的概念并重新查询模型，专门针对解释的"不完整性"问题，无需修改模型参数即可减少隐藏影响，提高LLM解释的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于 consequential 决策，其解释是审计模型行为的重要工具，但这些解释往往不忠实——存在不完整性（遗漏影响答案的因素）和不健全性（引用未影响答案的因素）。现有方法中，训练时方法需要访问模型权重和大量计算资源，测试时方法主要关注不健全性而非不完整性。

Method: 作者提出一种测试时方法针对性解决不完整性问题：从输入中移除解释中未提到的概念，然后用简化后的输入重新查询模型。这样消除了未提及因素的影响，同时保留了已提及概念的影响。该方法无需修改模型参数，适用于任何模型。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族和两个独立的忠实性度量指标上，该方法相比标准提示和鼓励忠实性的提示方法，均显著提升了解释的忠实性。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种模型无关的、可在推理时应用的有效机制，能够减少隐藏影响、提升LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性，为解释忠实性研究提供了针对"不完整性"维度的新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [216] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了一个"能力悖论"：提升大语言模型的个体能力可能反而降低系统级表现。由于共享训练与架构，更强大的LLM倾向于产生更相似（相关）的行为，这种相关性无法通过多样化消除，构成不可分散的风险底线。作者通过金融市场中的代理模拟实验验证了这一理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被大规模部署于金融、内容审核、招聘等关键现实系统，但现有研究主要关注个体模型能力提升，缺乏对系统级结果的考量。作者试图回答一个根本问题：提升个体能力是否必然带来更好的系统级表现？

Method: 作者提出了一个通用理论框架，论证模型间行为相关性如何产生不可分散的风险底线。随后在金融市场中构建基于代理的模拟实验，使用不同通用能力的LLM交易者进行测试，检验模型行为的相似性、相关性随能力的变化及其对市场风险的影响。

Result: 研究发现：（1)前沿LLM表现出显著相关的行为，且相关性随能力增强而增加；（2)当共享推理准确时，增加代理参与可降低市场级风险；（3)当代理处于共同的错误信息环境中时，相同的行为相关性反而成为负担，增加系统风险。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一个"能力悖论"：提升个体模型能力并不必然产生更好的系统级结果。LLM之间的相关性构成了一种非分散化风险，该发现对其他领域的适用性仍需经验验证，但为LLM系统设计与部署提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [217] [La Agente Óptima: Towards Agentic Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04564)
*Marcel Müller,Jiaru Bai,Willi Gottstein,Abhijoy Mandal,Mohammad Nazeri,Elia Savino,Yanlin Fang,Sujoy Das,Sergio Pablo García Carrillo,Yeonghun Kang,Juan B. Pérez-Sánchez,Simone Pilon,Martin Fitzner,Timothy Noël,Frank Gu,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了La Agente Óptima，一个基于LLM的智能体框架，用于构建和监督贝叶斯优化实验活动。该框架将LLM推理与实际执行的实验循环相分离，保持持久优化状态，能够在科学问题和执行环境演变时持续运行可执行的实验活动。在接触角优化和多目标流动化学等实际实验中表现优于人工指导的实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驾驶实验室（SDLs）结合自动化实验和自适应决策加速科学发现，但其运行通常依赖人类专家将科学目标转化为可执行的闭环实验活动并随数据和条件变化进行调整，这限制了SDLs的可扩展性和可及性。

Method: 提出La Agente Óptima智能体框架：将LLM推理与已执行的实验活动分离，使重复优化循环能持续一致地运行；仅在进展需要解释或活动修订时才将控制权返回给智能体；保持每个决策的可审计性。框架在消融研究、五个数字发现任务和两个物理平台上进行评估。

Result: 在闭环接触角优化实验中，Óptima识别并纠正了运行中期的测量故障，将接触角从71.4度优化至67.8度（目标64-66度），并正确推断出目标可能无法实现从而建议更换配方。在为期五天的多目标流动化学实验中，Óptima通过23次实验将产率从30%提升至59%，尽管推理成本较高，但总体成本更低、使用起始材料更少，并选择了更高效的工况点。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体能够使严格、长期的优化实验活动对领域科学家变得可及，无需专家设置，从而扩展了自驾驶实验室的应用范围。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) combine automated experimentation with adaptive decision-making to accelerate scientific discovery. Their operation nevertheless often depends on human specialists who translate scientific objectives into executable closed-loop campaigns. Specialists adjust them as data and operating conditions change. Here, we present La Agente Óptima, an agentic framework that constructs and supervises Bayesian optimization campaigns across computational and experimental systems while maintaining a persistent optimization state. By separating large language model (LLM) reasoning from executed campaigns, Óptima runs repetitive optimization loops consistently, returns control to the agent only when progress requires interpretation or campaign revision, and keeps every decision auditable. We evaluate Óptima across ablation studies, five digital discovery tasks, and two physical platforms. Throughout, Óptima maintained executable campaigns as both the scientific problem and execution environment evolved. In a closed-loop contact angle optimization campaign, Óptima identified and corrected a mid-run measurement failure, bringing the contact angle from 71.4 to 67.8 degrees, just above the 64-66 degree range. From this result, Óptima correctly inferred that the target was likely unattainable with the available reagents and recommended changing the formulation. In a five-day multi-objective flow-chemistry campaign, Óptima increased the yield from 30% to 59% over 23 experiments. Despite substantial inference costs, it cost less and used substantially less starting material than a human-directed campaign, while selecting a more mass-efficient operating point. These results show that LLM-based agents can make rigorous, long-running optimization campaigns accessible to domain scientists without specialist setup, expanding the scope of SDLs.

</details>


### [218] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，通过五个能力平面和四大架构支柱，将企业结构化、非结构化、多模态和隐性知识转化为本体锚定的企业基础，实现推理与受控执行的组合，并在巴西JCI认证三级医院中验证了六个成熟度阶段中的三个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败并非源于模型能力不足，而在于组织缺乏编码其决策、谈判和执行方式的结构化基座。通用LLM不携带公司特定本体先验，RAG脆弱且无法通向可执行动作，静态剧本虽编码逻辑但无法推理和适应，因此需要从设计之初就将隐性知识捕获、本体锚定、主权部署和可审计执行协同设计。

Method: CLM框架包含五大能力平面和四大架构支柱：神经符号网格（Neurosymbolic Mesh）将生成模型与知识图谱耦合；技能图（Skill Graph）对可复用策略、人物角色、异议和目标进行类型化组合；活体数字孪生（Living Digital Twins）将功能领域建模为推理代理；深层安全层（Deep Security Layer）执行主权、可追踪性和人类监督。同时采用Spec-as-Code范式连接锚定意图与可执行工件。

Result: CLM被定义为独立研究对象；引入技能图实现可解释性的组合式构建；提出"智慧聆听者"效应，即具备隐性能力的基础设施随使用而价值复合增长，与动态能力和组织学习相关联；来自巴西JCI认证三级医院的证据（在LGPD合规下）实例化了六个成熟度阶段中的三个。

Conclusion: CLM代表了基础中心类架构的一个具体实例化，证明企业AI的可行路径不在于更强的通用模型，而在于构建能够捕获、锚定并复合组织知识的企业基础。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [219] [From Language Models to World-Acting Systems: Progress and Limits of Agentic AI across Digital, Social, Virtual, and Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04894)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对截至2026年8月的大语言模型智能体研究进行批判性综述，提出"合理授权"作为分析启发式，强调在证据不足的情况下不应将能力扩展等同于自主性进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为智能体的能力扩展常被叙述为通往自主性的单向进程，混淆了模型能力、系统集成、持久性和安全授权等概念，缺乏系统性的批判审视。

Method: 综合截至2026年8月31日的主要研究论文与官方技术规范，沿"委托授权、时间持久性、环境耦合"三个维度组织证据，并将模型、工具（harness）与环境进行分离分析。

Result: 证据显示动作-接口扩展比稳健完成、恢复、授权或独立验证更有据可查；MCP和A2A协议促进了互操作性但未建立可信委托；多智能体带来专精化但伴随成本和关联失效；持久模拟与物理机器人仅证明有限可行性而非无人值守的开放世界可靠性。

Conclusion: 提出"合理授权"作为规范性启发式：仅在具备溯源、受限授权、故障检测、安全恢复和校准人类控制等证据支持的情况下扩展动作范围，并据此提出模型-工具耦合评估、基于能力权限、持久状态、跨智能体问责及分阶段物理验证的研究议程。

Abstract: Large language models become consequential agents when surrounding systems let outputs change external state. Models now call tools, operate interfaces, delegate work, retain state, inhabit generated worlds, and control robots or laboratory equipment. Such advances are often narrated as one march toward autonomy, conflating model competence, system integration, persistence, and safe authority. This critical review synthesizes primary research and official technical specifications available by 31 August 2026. We organize the evidence along delegated authority, temporal persistence, and environmental coupling, while separating model, harness, and environment. Within the evidence examined, action-interface expansion is documented more convincingly than robust completion, recovery, authorization, or independent verification. Model Context Protocol and Agent2Agent improve interoperability but do not establish trustworthy delegation; multi-agent organization adds specialization alongside cost and correlated failure. Persistent simulations and world models support training and planning but do not themselves demonstrate agency; robotics and self-driving laboratories establish bounded feasibility rather than unattended open-world reliability. We propose justified delegation as an analytical and normative heuristic, not an observed law or certified score: expand action scope only where evidence supports provenance, bounded authority, failure detection, safe recovery, and calibrated human control. This framing yields a research agenda for coupled model-harness evaluation, capability-based permissions, durable state, cross-agent accountability, and staged physical validation.

</details>


### [220] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench是首个将避免副作用进行"定价"并明确命名副作用对象为"生物"的基准测试。它通过一个农场仿真环境（两辆拖拉机协同收割玉米，田野中有动物），测量LLM子代理在无记忆、无伤害提示的决策条件下，为避开动物愿意付出多少燃料价格代价。实验涉及9个模型和7,201次定价决策，显示杀生率从0.4%到98.8%不等，且与模型能力无关；价格敏感性和道德简报等变量对行为有显著影响。该基准无需LLM评分器，完全可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准虽然能测量智能体实现目标过程中的副作用，但没有衡量避免副作用的"代价"（即模型愿意付出多少来避免伤害），也未将副作用明确命名为"生物"。作者希望超越模型"口头表态"的局限性，通过价格机制量化模型避免伤害的真实意愿。

Method: 设计一个农场收割仿真环境（HarvestBench），其中两辆拖拉机受到LLM子代理控制进行合作收割。环境采用强化学习网格世界，每次决策无记忆，目标中不提及伤害。当动物阻挡路线时，自动驾驶停车询问模型：继续行驶（无燃料成本，会杀死动物）还是绕行（支付一定的燃料价格）。对照组包括岩石（损坏拖拉机）和干草捆（无害非生物）。另设从邻居田里取作物的测试作为第二条道德判断线。评分器直接从游戏日志计数事件，不依赖LLM评分。

Result: 在9个模型和7,201次定价决策中，3,951次涉及动物。杀生率从0.4%（Terra和Sol最仁慈）到98.8%（GPT-4o-mini最残忍）不等，且与能力无关。9个模型中6个对价格敏感（5%显著性水平），弹性范围0.09-1.69。所有9个模型在默认地图上压过野生动物的频率高于农场动物。道德简报影响最大：6个推理模型中有5个在道德简报下杀生率低于6%，移除简报后6个模型全部超过84%。

Conclusion: HarvestBench以可复现、无LLM评分器的方式衡量了模型为避免伤害愿意"支付"的真实代价，而非其对伤害的表述。结果表明模型的道德行为与其能力无关，且高度受提示语（简报）影响，价格弹性在不同模型间差异显著。该基准揭示了模型在道德决策上的真实行为模式及其脆弱性。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [221] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验生产环境多智能体系统中"角色可互换"假设，通过在多个团队间交换角色匹配的智能体，发现换人对任务分数影响小，但显著降低通信效率（增加16%-63%），且形成历史越长团队漂移越大，交换代价越高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产多智能体系统假设同一角色可被任意能胜任该工作的智能体替换，但该假设未经验证。作者希望测试这一核心假设是否成立，探究团队内部协作中形成的隐性知识（如惯例）是否导致智能体不可完全互换。

Method: 每个环境中从同一基座模型独立形成8个团队，在相同任务上运行，每个智能体在10次组建episode中保留私人记事本；然后在团队间交换角色匹配的智能体，并在留出任务上测量变化。设置安慰剂对照以排除阵容变动本身的干扰效应，通过三种消融实验（基座模型、解码温度、形成时长）分析交换惩罚的相关因素。

Result: 交换智能体对任务分数影响很小，但单位进展所需的通信量增加16%-63%；Hanabi中被交换的智能体比无经验智能体更"昂贵"，表明与其前搭档学到的惯例产生干扰；Collab-Overcooked中当设定议程的智能体被替换时，额外通信主要来自留下的智能体；三种消融显示交换惩罚与独立团队间的漂移程度相关——贪心解码降低两者，而加倍团队历史提高两者。

Conclusion: 在任务结果上智能体比在协作效率上更具可互换性。团队形成历史越长，独立形成的团队漂移越大，交换代价也越高，表明协作中的隐性约定（惯例）形成是交换代价的主要来源。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [222] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PerfReasoning基准，评估LLM在性能建模中的两种能力：直接性能推理和生成分析性性能模型代码。发现闭源模型在推理问答上表现优异（>90%），但模型构建任务对所有模型都极具挑战性（除GPT-5.6 Sol外平均低于15%），并验证了任务特定RL的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模对硬件设计和软件优化至关重要，但构建这些模型需要对计算、数据重用、存储和数据移动进行结构化推理。目前缺乏系统评估LLM在性能建模领域能力的基准，包括直接推理和代码生成两个维度。

Method: 构建PerfReasoning基准，给定工作负载、架构和映射规格，要求模型比较映射方案并预测片外流量和缓冲区需求。评估LLM作为直接性能推理器和分析性能模型代码生成器两种角色，并测试任务特定RL和反馈无关的多轮自我修正提示策略。

Result: 最强闭源模型在推理问答上超过90%，最佳开源模型达82.4%。模型构建任务上GPT-5.6 Sol超过80%通过率，但其他模型配置平均低于15%且运行间差异大。任务特定RL将4B模型的映射推理准确率提升15.7个百分点，而多轮自我修正提示不可靠。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning揭示了LLM在架构性推理的合理表现与可靠性能模型构建之间存在的显著差距，基准将公开发布以支持可复现评估并追踪未来进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [223] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出"循环状态写回"（recurrent-state write-back）概念，系统研究了量化循环神经网络中状态存储规则对推理精度的影响。通过在荧光寿命成像的GRU编码器-解码器任务中实验，发现将连续状态替换为4位确定性存储会使参数估计误差增大70倍和300倍，并提出误差反馈、残差记忆、方向记忆等方法在不重训练的情况下恢复精度，同时验证该现象在LSTM中同样存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化虽被广泛用于降低神经网络推理的计算和内存开销，但循环神经网络中量化后的状态会在下一时间步被存储并回读，因此状态存储规则可能改变后续计算。此前研究未系统分析这一"状态写回"规则对低精度循环动力学的影响，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在紧凑GRU编码器-解码器上执行荧光寿命成像任务（估计τ1和τ2两个寿命参数），保持训练好的模型不变，将连续状态传播替换为确定性4位状态存储，对比精度变化；进一步提出误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆方法来传递被抑制更新的信息；在不同精度下进行扫描，并进行匹配训练对比；最后在独立训练的LSTM上重复后训练干预以验证泛化性。

Result: 确定性4位状态存储使τ1和τ2的估计误差分别增大约70倍和300倍；失败原因是重复的小更新低于写阈值导致状态几乎不变而网络持续提议变化；误差反馈等方法无需重训练即可恢复精度；提高状态精度可能恶化固定循环解，而匹配训练可学习状态接口兼容性；LSTM中同样复现了粗写回失败，误差反馈可恢复精度，且细胞状态比隐藏状态对状态特定干预更敏感。

Conclusion: 循环状态写回是低精度循环动力学的关键决定因素，状态存储接口应成为量化循环推理的核心设计考量。本文揭示的失败模式和恢复机制为量化RNN的实际部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned. To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [224] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ResLearn-XR，一个用于XR网络流量预测和QoE风险评估的残差学习框架，采用两阶段时间学习结构，在数值空间进行流量预测、在logit空间进行QoE风险估计，并引入Data Descriptor Algorithm进行加密流量特征构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR网络流量具有突发性、非平稳性的动态特征，现有方法难以同时实现准确的流量预测和QoE风险评估，需要一种能够适应这些动态特性的框架。

Method: ResLearn-XR采用两阶段时间学习结构：基础序列预测模型结合任务特化残差学习组件。残差学习在数值空间用于连续流量预测，在logit空间用于概率QoE风险估计。QoE分支引入Data Descriptor Algorithm（DDA），一种因果特征构建模块，将数据包级应用层观测转换为帧时序感知的描述符，适用于加密流量分析。同时构建配对XR流量与QoE标签的数据集。

Result: 在帧计数、帧大小和到达间隔时间预测上将SMAPE降低最高17.84%；在QoE风险评估上将SMAPE较单阶段基线降低最高87.8%。

Conclusion: ResLearn-XR通过任务特化残差学习有效提升了XR网络流量预测精度与QoE风险估计性能，残差学习结合基础模型的结构优于传统单阶段基线方法。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [225] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将间接提示注入形式化为测试时搜索问题，并引入了一个具有专用搜索框架的代理型攻击者，该攻击者执行环境侦察、结构化推理和基于受害者代理反馈的自适应评估。研究发现，增加攻击者的测试时计算量能提升漏洞发现与利用能力，且显式策略管理对避免冗余搜索至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估往往将攻击成功视为受害者的固有属性，而忽略了攻击者搜索过程与计算预算对漏洞发现的影响。作者旨在提出一个系统性框架来理解代理型攻击者如何通过自适应搜索来利用工具使用型代理系统的安全漏洞。

Method: 将间接提示注入形式化为任务相关的攻击面搜索问题；构建代理型攻击者，配备专用搜索框架（包括环境侦察、结构化攻击策略推理、基于受害者反馈的自适应评估）；在异构任务上进行实验，包括计算预算缩放实验和消融研究。

Result: 增加攻击者测试时计算量可提升漏洞发现与利用效果；消融实验表明显式策略管理对于避免冗余搜索和在更大预算下维持性能增益非常重要；发现攻击者的自适应搜索是关键安全风险。

Conclusion: 安全评估应同时刻画攻击者的搜索过程与计算预算，而非将攻击成功率视为独立于预算的受害者属性。攻击者针对系统攻击面的自适应搜索是工具使用型代理中一个重要且未被充分探索的安全风险。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [226] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync是一个将可穿戴与移动设备的多模态生理信号（心脏、神经、行为、语音）整合为连续复合数字生物标志物（BSI）的模型，采用多头自注意力处理模态标记并添加线性分支。在两个合成队列上，其性能与简单特征拼接相当或略优，且对模态丢失更具鲁棒性，但尚需真实队列验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴和移动设备提供的各类生理测量（心、神经、行为和语音）都是部分且含噪的生理状态视图，单一模态信息有限，联合观测可能包含各模态单独无法获取的信息，因此需要一个能有效融合多种模态的复合数字生物标志物。

Method: BioSync对模态标记应用多头自注意力机制，并添加一个假设类包含标准特征拼接的线性分支，构成"宽而深"的架构。该设计由潜在变量测量理论和联合观测信息论据驱动，遵循BEST框架定义BSI为连续复合数字生物标志物。评估使用两个文献启发的合成队列：四模态认知衰退队列（HRV、EEG、活动记录和语音）以及基于公共AI-READI可穿戴模式构建的代谢-自主神经队列。

Result: 在认知队列中BioSync与拼接方法AUC分别达0.928和0.926；在代谢队列中准确率/F1分别达0.764/0.766（拼接为0.756/0.758）。BSI与潜在严重度相关系数为0.91和0.68。纯注意力消融在认知队列AUC为0.911，说明宽深融合贡献了性能提升。在模态丢失训练下，BioSync在认知队列六种损坏率中五种及代谢队列最高损坏率下优于拼接。其认知队列AUC也高于五个发表的数字生物标志物参考值。

Conclusion: BioSync提供了一种有理论动机的多模态融合框架用于复合数字生物标志物构建，在合成队列中表现出与基准相当或更优的性能，尤其在模态数据缺损时更具鲁棒性。然而，与简单拼接的性能差异有限，且目前仅在合成数据上验证，必须在真实队列中进一步验证其临床效用。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [227] [What Does Multi-Harness RL Learn? Credit Assignment and Portability in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04518)
*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在仓库级代码任务中隔离研究了多执行框架强化学习中"跨框架相对优势分组"（Cross）与"单框架分组"（Within）两种规则的影响。结果发现：评估执行框架是主导变量（使求解率从2.14%到9.27%，相差4.3倍），而分组规则的影响微乎其微（Cross与Within在留出框架上差异仅+0.25pp，且置信区间跨越0）。两种规则的种子方差（0.42-0.45pp）甚至超过两者间的差异。跨框架信用分配只带来配置适配，不产生更强的可迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多执行框架强化学习已成为Agent训练的主要范式，通常包含两个设计选择：策略暴露于多个执行框架、以及在相对优势分组中比较奖励。以往研究将两者混为一谈，未能厘清"跨框架分组比较奖励"这一选择是否真正带来了能力的提升，还是仅仅由评估框架的差异所主导。

Method: 从Qwen3-8B监督微调起点出发，在同一批冻结的任务-框架记录（来自Aider、OpenHands、Qwen Code和SWE-agent四个执行框架）上重放相同更新次数的GRPO训练，对比两种分组规则：Within（每任务-框架对一组）与Cross（同一任务内框架池化分组）。用封存的SWE-bench Verified评审器对24,000次评估的所有检查点打分，包括四个训练框架和一个训练时未见的极简框架。

Result: 评估框架将平均求解率从2.14%提升至9.27%（4.3倍），训练方案仅贡献1.16的变化。分组规则无显著影响：Cross与Within在留出框架上差异为+0.25pp（95%CI: [-0.48, +1.02]）。每种规则自身的种子波动范围（0.42-0.45pp）超过两规则间差异。分类器能从Cross的pooled优势（超出打乱标签基线+4.48pp）恢复生成框架，但从Within不能。两种规则在每个框架内的动作分布和留出框架得分相同。收集一半on-policy训练数据不改变结论。

Conclusion: 跨执行框架的信用分配仅带来配置适配（适应训练所用框架的特性），而不产生比框架内信用分配更可移植的能力。多执行框架RL研究报告应明确说明分组边界，并在未见过的执行框架上进行测试。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding. From one Qwen3-8B supervised warm start we replay the same frozen task-harness records from Aider, OpenHands, Qwen Code, and SWE-agent, with the same number of updates, under two rules for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), Within (one group per task-harness pair) and Cross (harnesses pooled within a task), and score every checkpoint with a sealed SWE-bench Verified oracle on four source harnesses and a minimal harness held out of training. The evaluation harness is the dominant variable: across 24,000 sealed evaluations it moves the mean solve rate from 2.14\% to 9.27\%, a factor of 4.3, where the training recipe moves it by 1.16. The grouping rule is not. On the held-out harness, Cross minus Within is +0.25 pp, 95\% confidence interval [-0.48, +1.02], at eight attempts per task, and +0.16 [-0.41, +0.72] pooled over three training seeds whose individual estimates change sign. Each rule's own seed range, 0.42 to 0.45 pp, exceeds the difference between them. Both rules place their largest gains on the same source harness. The pooled advantage carries the harness: an out-of-fold classifier recovers the generating harness from Cross's advantage +4.48 pp above the shuffled-label baseline and from Within's not at all, and the two rules still reach the same held-out score and action distribution inside each harness. Re-collecting half the training data on-policy does not change this. Cross-harness credit yields configuration adaptation and no more portable capability than within-harness credit. Multi-harness RL reports should state the grouping boundary and test under an unseen harness.

</details>


### [228] [MaxKernel: Agentic Kernel Generation for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04523)
*Shangkun Wang,Nina Cai,Charles Hoong,Julian Walker,Gerson Kroiz,George Vanica,Deepak Patil,Andi Gavrilescu,Hassan Sipra,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaxKernel 是一个面向 TPU 内核开发的多智能体系统，实现了三种范式：人机协同（HITL）、全自动优化（Auto）和图基自主搜索，在 50 个 TPU 内核任务和真实模型负载上均能匹敌专家手写基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速器编写高性能自定义内核需要深厚的硬件级专业知识，传统手工开发成本高且难度大，因此希望利用大语言模型结合编译器实时反馈来构建智能体系统，自动生成高质量内核。

Method: 提出 MaxKernel 多智能体系统，包含三种开发范式：HITL 智能体支持逐步协作设计；Auto 智能体执行全自动的基于指标/追踪的优化循环；Graph-Based Autonomous Search 扩展 Auto 智能体以进行全局设计空间探索。三种范式共享专业子智能体池，负责任务规划、实现、自调试、测试和硬件性能剖析。

Result: 在 JaxBench（含 50 个多样化 TPU 内核任务）及开源 SOTA 模型的复杂真实工作负载上评估，MaxKernel 持续生成高度优化的实现，与专家手调基线性能匹配并表现显著优越。

Conclusion: MaxKernel 证明了基于 LLM 的多智能体系统结合实时编译器反馈能够有效自动生成高性能 TPU 内核，达到专家级水平；系统已开源。

Abstract: Designing and authoring high-performance custom kernels for accelerators is a complex task that requires deep hardware-level expertise. Large Language Models (LLM) can be leveraged together with real-time compiler feedback to build agentic systems for kernel generation. In this work, we present MaxKernel, a multi-agent system that implements three distinct paradigms for TPU kernel development: (1) a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) agent for collaborative, step-by-step design; (2) an Autonomous (Auto) agent that executes a fully automated, metric/trace-driven optimization loop; and (3) a Graph-Based Autonomous Search that scales the Auto agent for global exploration of the design space. All three paradigms leverage a shared pool of specialized sub-agents to handle planning, implementation, self-debugging, testing, and hardware profiling. We evaluate MaxKernel on JaxBench, a comprehensive suite of 50 diverse kernel tasks for TPUs, alongside complex, real-world workloads from state-of-the-art open-source models. We demonstrate that MaxKernel consistently generates highly optimized implementations, matching expert hand-tuned baselines and delivering significant performance across the benchmark. Our agent is open-sourced and available https://github.com/AI-Hypercomputer/accelerator-agents/tree/main/MaxKernel.

</details>


### [229] [Towards a universal language of concepts: A survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04528)
*Aishni Parab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序是概念学习的通用表示语言，并回顾了使用程序作为概念表示的各类计算模型，评估其对构建通用表示语言的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可以从少量数据中学习和泛化新概念，因为人类以丰富的结构化格式表达知识。研究者希望找到一种能够作为概念通用表示的语言，而程序被认为是一个强有力的候选方案。

Method: 本文采用综述的方法，回顾了使用程序作为概念表示的计算模型，并从通用表示语言的角度对这些模型进行了系统评估与分析。

Result: 通过对现有使用程序作为概念表示的学习模型的梳理和评估，分析它们在迈向通用表示语言这一目标上的贡献与不足。

Conclusion: 程序作为概念表示的候选语言具有潜力，但要实现真正通用的表示语言仍需进一步探索和发展。

Abstract: Humans can learn and generalize novel concepts from sparse data because they express knowledge in rich structural formats. In this paper, we propose that programs are a strong candidate for universal representation of concepts. We review computational models of concept learning that use programs as their concept representation and evaluate their contribution toward a universal representational language.

</details>


### [230] [Data-Driven Discovery of Composition-Dependent Constitutive Models for Hyperelasticity and Viscoelasticity of Digital Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04541)
*Josué García-Ávila,Beijun Shen,Manuel K. Rausch,Mary C. Boyce,Adrián Buganza-Tepole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架，该框架保留了Bergström-Boyce经典粘弹性模型的结构（乘性运动学、基于不变量的应变能函数和标量耗散演化律），通过神经微分方程或人工神经网络自动化构建多材料3D打印数字材料的本构模型，在保持热力学一致性的同时捕捉跨材料成分的率相关刚度和迟滞行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多材料3D打印的数字材料表现出极为非线性的、成分依赖且率相关的耗散行为。经典有限应变粘弹性模型依赖封闭形式的应变能函数和内变量演化，当单一本构模型需跨材料和加载率泛化时灵活性受限，因此需要更灵活的数据驱动建模方法。

Method: 该框架基于Bergström-Boyce公式进行推广：平衡分支通过数据驱动发现方法直接预测封闭形式模型参数，或利用神经微分方程（NODEs）自动构造多凸应变能函数；非平衡分支动力学类似地通过跨成分直接识别封闭参数或使用约束人工神经网络学习。利用多速率单轴压缩数据（多种材料成分）进行训练。

Result: 该公式能够跨多种材料成分捕获率相关的刚度和迟滞，同时保持热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动多材料本构建模框架成功将经典粘弹性模型结构与机器学习数据发现相结合，在保障热力学一致性的前提下实现了对复杂多材料数字材料行为的准确泛化建模。

Abstract: Digital materials fabricated by multi-material 3D printing are designed as controlled mixtures of stiff and compliant constituents, yielding effective responses that span more than an order of magnitude in apparent stiffness and exhibit strongly nonlinear, composition-dependent, and rate-dependent dissipative behavior. Classical finite-strain viscoelastic models represent such behavior with closed-form strain energy functions for equilibrium and non-equilibrium stresses as well as evolution of internal variables, which may limit flexibility when a single constitutive model is expected to generalize across materials and loading rates. Here, we present a data-driven multi-material constitutive modeling framework that generalizes a formulation by Bergström and Boyce. The proposed framework retains the structure of the classical model, namely multiplicative kinematics, invariant-based strain-energy functions, and a scalar dissipative evolution law directed along the normalized nonequilibrium deviatoric stress. For the equilibrium branch, the data-driven discovery framework either directly predicts closed-form model parameters as functions of composition or automatically constructs a polyconvex strain-energy function using neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs). The nonequilibrium branch kinetics are learned similarly, either by directly identifying closed-form parameters across compositions or by using appropriately constrained artificial neural networks. Using multi-rate uniaxial compression data across multiple material compositions, we show that the proposed formulation captures rate-dependent stiffness and hysteresis across compositions while preserving thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [231] [From Answers to Interpretations: Rethinking Ambiguity-Induced Aleatoric Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04543)
*Omer Nahum,Niv Nayman,Jonathan Fhima,Alon Zolfi,Jeremy Levy,Shai Mazor,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于澄清（clarification）而不需要答案的歧义诱导偶然不确定性估计方法，相比传统回答比较方法在AUROC、计算成本和与认知不确定性相关性上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型部署中，区分不确定性是来自任务的固有可变性（偶然不确定性）还是模型知识局限（认知不确定性）至关重要。现有方法通过生成多个输入澄清、查询答案并比较来估计偶然不确定性，但作者认为答案并非必要，可能冗余、昂贵且会引入认知泄漏。

Method: 提出一种仅依赖澄清空间的方法，不从澄清后的输入生成答案，而是直接从可能的解释空间中估计由输入歧义引起的偶然不确定性分量。作者从理论上论证了答案不需要且可能产生误导，并在三个基准上将歧义检测作为操作性评估任务。

Result: 该方法在AUROC上达到63.34（对比基线60.85），输出token成本降低4-26倍，API调用降低2.2-3.5倍，且估计结果与认知不确定性的相关性显著更低。

Conclusion: 歧义诱导的偶然不确定性更适合从解释空间中估计，而非从响应空间中估计，这为更高效且更可靠的不确定性量化提供了新方向。

Abstract: A key challenge in reliable LLM deployment is recognizing when uncertainty reflects irreducible variability in the task rather than limitations in the model's knowledge. In language tasks, a central source of such aleatoric uncertainty is input ambiguity or underspecification, where multiple interpretations remain plausible. Existing decomposition methods estimate aleatoric uncertainty by generating multiple clarifications of the input, querying the model for an answer under each clarification, and comparing the resulting answers. We argue that answers are not necessary for identifying ambiguity: they are often redundant, add avoidable cost, and can mislead through epistemic leakage. We support this claim theoretically, and propose a clarification-only approach that estimates this ambiguity-induced component directly from the space of plausible interpretations, without answers to the clarified inputs. Using ambiguity detection as an operational evaluation across three benchmarks, this direct approach improves AUROC (63.34 vs. 60.85), reduces computational cost by 4-26x in output tokens and 2.2-3.5x in API calls, and yields estimates with substantially lower correlation with epistemic uncertainty. Overall, our results suggest that ambiguity-induced aleatoric uncertainty is better estimated from the interpretation space than from the response space.

</details>


### [232] [IPGeoAI: Transformer-Based Geolocation with LLM Semantic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04559)
*Avinash Kadimisetty,Andy Jinqing Yu,Philip Favaloro,Wenlong Liu,Xiaolu Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IPGeoAI，一种基于Transformer编码器的深度学习模型，将城市级IP地理定位从静态查找重构为序列建模任务。该模型通过零样本LLM特征提取将AS描述转化为结构化的语义元数据，并利用多头交叉注意力机制融合语义信号与网络拓扑信息，在私有数据集（覆盖20万城市）上显著优于外部厂商方案，并在在线生产环境中取得+0.35%的下游指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式方法和数据库驱动的IP地理定位技术难以应对现代网络基础设施中复杂非线性的IP分配模式，尤其是在IPv6地址空间迅速扩展和移动网络高度动态的背景下。现有方法无法有效捕获IP子网结构中的层次依赖关系，也难以消解地理位置的模糊性。

Method: 1) 利用Transformer Encoder对IP子网结构中的层次依赖关系进行建模，将地理定位转化为序列建模任务；2) 提出Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline，通过预计算将嘈杂的AS描述转换为结构化的领域元数据（如大学vs ISP、全球vs本地）；3) 通过多头交叉注意力模块将语义信号与数值网络拓扑进行融合；4) 采用分层推理策略，从粗粒度国家信号出发逐步细化到城市级定位。

Result: 在覆盖20万城市的私有数据集离线评估中，IPGeoAI显著优于领先的外部厂商方案；通过分层推理策略，城市级准确率提升6%，同时覆盖范围扩展至100%流量。在大型在线生产测试中，模型推动了第一梯队下游用例指标统计学显著的+0.35%提升。

Conclusion: IPGeoAI验证了将深度序列建模与LLM语义特征相结合可以有效提升城市级IP地理定位精度，弥合了数值网络拓扑与现实语义身份之间的鸿沟，为复杂的现代网络环境中的地理定位问题提供了新颖且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate city-level IP Geolocation is an important enabler for the modern digital ecosystem, underpinning services ranging from local content delivery and targeting to digital rights enforcement. However, traditional heuristic and database-driven methods often struggle to resolve the complex, non-linear allocation patterns of modern network infrastructures, particularly within the exploding IPv6 address space and transient mobile networks. In this paper, we introduce IPGeoAI, a novel deep learning model architecture that reframes geolocation from a static lookup problem to a sequential modeling task. Our approach utilizes the Transformer Encoder to capture hierarchical dependencies inherent in IP subnet structures. We propose a method to resolve geographic ambiguity by integrating unstructured semantic context via a Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline. We utilize Large Language Models to transform raw, noisy Autonomous Systems (AS) descriptions into structured, domain-specific metadata (such as 'University' vs. 'ISP' or 'Global' vs. 'Local') via an offline pre-computation process. By fusing these semantic signals into the network via a Multi-Head Cross-Attention module, we bridge the gap between numerical network topology and real-world semantic identity. Extensive offline evaluation on a proprietary dataset spanning 200,000 cities demonstrates that IPGeoAI significantly outperforms a leading external vendor in city-level granularity. By adopting a hierarchical inference strategy that refines coarse-grained country signals, our model achieves a 6% improvement in city-level accuracy while extending coverage to 100% of the traffic. Furthermore, in large-scale online production tests, the model drove a statistically significant +0.35% improvement in our 1st-tier downstream use cases metric.

</details>


### [233] [Reducing Hallucinated Transcripts in Whisper via Hallucination Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04561)
*Maryam Abbasihafshejani,Murtuza Jadliwala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推理时方法，通过对解码器激活进行低秩投影来抑制Whisper语音识别模型在无语音输入时的幻觉转录。在非语音基准上，始终开启的投影将平均幻觉率从31.31%降至2.44%（相对降低92.21%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper作为广泛使用的语音识别基础模型，其生成式解码器在处理很少或没有语音的输入时会产生流畅的幻觉转录，这一问题需要在不重新训练模型的前提下得到解决。

Method: 从非语音校准数据中估计一个紧凑的幻觉相关子空间，通过低秩投影将解码器隐藏状态从该子空间中投影出去。提出了两种变体：always-on（对所有输入应用投影）和gated（仅在模型预测输入可能为非语音时应用投影）。

Result: 在非语音基准上，always-on投影将平均幻觉率从31.31%降至2.44%（相对减少92.21%），gated投影降至3.74%（相对减少88.05%）。在LibriSpeech上，gated投影使绝对词错误率增加0.33-4.39个百分点，误拒率达0.41%-9.97%。

Conclusion: 低秩激活投影能够在不重新训练的情况下大幅抑制Whisper的幻觉，同时在幻觉抑制与语音识别性能之间提供了可控的权衡。

Abstract: Whisper is a widely used foundation model for automatic speech recognition (ASR), but its generative decoder can produce fluent hallucinated transcripts for inputs containing little or no speech. We propose a training-free, inference-time method to reduce these hallucinations using low-rank projection of decoder activations. A compact hallucination-associated subspace is estimated from non-speech calibration data, and decoder hidden states are projected away from this subspace during inference.
  We evaluate two variants: always-on, which applies projection to all inputs, and gated, which applies it only when Whisper predicts that an input is likely non-speech. Across non-speech benchmarks, always-on projection reduces average hallucination rate (HR) from 31.31% to 2.44%, a 92.21% relative reduction, while gated projection reduces HR to 3.74%, an 88.05% relative reduction, with lower false rejection of genuine speech. On LibriSpeech, gated projection increases absolute word error rate (WER) by 0.33-4.39 percentage points and yields false-rejection rates (FRR) of 0.41--9.97% across model and split settings.
  These results show that low-rank activation projection can substantially suppress Whisper hallucinations without retraining, while providing a controllable trade-off between hallucination suppression and speech recognition performance.

</details>


### [234] [Extremely Sparse Supervision Incentivizes Reasoning Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04565)
*Zhishuai Liu,Xingzi Xu,Mehmet Saygin Seyfioglu,Pan Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现在on-policy蒸馏（OPD）设置下，仅用每条推理轨迹中极少量的token（如1-2个，占全部token的0.05%）作为监督信号，就能匹配甚至超越全token训练对推理能力的提升效果。该现象在多个模型规模、多种任务和不同训练方法中一致出现，挑战了"有效的后训练必须依赖大量token"的传统假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法隐式假设有效的学习必须是大规模token密集型的，但这一假设缺乏充分验证。作者希望重新审视这一假设，探究是否可以通过极稀疏的监督信号来实现有效的推理能力提升，从而为更高效的后训练算法设计提供新的思路。

Method: 在on-policy distillation（OPD）设置下，利用Qwen3模型家族，构建九组不同规模的teacher-student配置，仅对每条推理轨迹中极少量的关键token（如1-2个）施加训练监督，并与全token训练进行对比。实验涵盖数学推理、代码推理、Llama模型以及基于PPO的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）等多种设置进行验证。

Result: 极稀疏的token监督（仅0.05%的token）在大多数情况下能够匹配或超越全token训练对推理能力的提升效果。该现象在九个teacher-student配置、不同模型规模、数学推理和代码推理任务、Llama模型以及PPO-based RLVR方法中均得到一致验证。

Conclusion: 有效的后训练不一定需要token密集型的监督。极稀疏的监督可能更接近人类自然的学习过程——不是逐词纠正，而是反思关键的推理步骤并更新理解。这一发现挑战了现有假设，为设计更高效的后训练算法指明了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models demonstrate increasingly strong reasoning capabilities through effective post-training. Yet, prevailing post-training methods optimize over massive numbers of tokens, implicitly assuming that effective learning must be token-intensive. We revisit this assumption in the on-policy distillation (OPD) setting, which naturally admits dense teacher supervision at every generated token. Using the Qwen3 family, we discover a counter-intuitive phenomenon: reasoning can be effectively incentivized by an extremely small fraction of generated tokens--as few as one or two tokens per reasoning trajectory, corresponding to only 0.05% of all tokens. Surprisingly, this sparse supervision in most cases matches or surpasses full-token training in improving reasoning ability, despite excluding the vast majority of generated tokens from the training objective. This phenomenon is consistently observed across nine teacher--student configurations spanning different model scales on mathematical reasoning tasks, and is further validated on coding reasoning, Llama models and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based reinforcement learning with verifiable reward (RLVR). Interestingly, such extremely sparse supervision may be closer to the natural learning process: rather than correcting every step word by word, one reflects on a few critical reasoning steps, updates prior understanding, and continues the trial-and-error, avoiding micro-level corrections while remaining remarkably effective. Overall, our results challenge the assumption that effective post-training must be token-intensive and point to a new direction for understanding and designing more efficient post-training algorithms.

</details>


### [235] [Does the Selected Object Reach the Reader? Auditing Identity Handoffs in Grounded Language-Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04579)
*Siddharth Vohra,Runmin Jiang,Xiaomo Li,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"返回对象概览（ROP）"框架，揭示混合问答流水线中"选中对象"与"数据集追踪的段落基准"在身份上并不总是一致，并通过大规模审计（600个HybridQA问题、1,463~1,792条可解析记录）量化了这种不一致对检索评估和读者端性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准召回率检查的是数据集关联的段落对象，但实际流水线中读者依赖的是"被选中的对象经检索后返回"的结果。如果选中对象在传递环节丢失，则读者无法获得证据。然而，这两个对象（选中对象与数据集追踪段落的基准对象）在身份上可能不同，导致基准指标失真，这一关键偏差未被充分研究。

Method: 对600个HybridQA问题运用三类选择器家族进行审计。设计了可解析记录（resolvable records）体系，比较精确键值查找、精确标题匹配、BM25正文检索（top-5）、混合检索+重排序等多种检索规则。通过冻结读者模型对比实验，衡量对齐对象存在与否对精确匹配分数的影响，并构造64项精选队列进行消融验证（移除该段落vs移除相似长度对照段落）。最终发布ROP数据集，包含目标对象、返回ID字段、截断值、成员规则和完整预期种群的可执行记录。

Result: 在1,463条"选中对象与数据集追踪段落匹配"的记录中，精确键值和精确标题检索每次都成功返回对象；BM25仅限正文时在389条记录（26.6%）遗漏（截断值为5）；混合检索+重排序仅遗漏14条（1.0%）。两个身份在329/1,792条可解析记录上不一致。基于原始问题排名时，两者的top-5检查在106条记录（5.9%）上不一致。对齐对象的存在带来28.6~31.0点的精确匹配提升；在64项精选中，移除该段落显著降低精确匹配，而移除相似长度对照段落无此效应。

Conclusion: 基准对象与流水线实际所需对象的身份偏差是一个被忽视但影响显著的评估盲区。作者通过ROP工具包提供可复现的审计手段，为未来混合问答流水线提供更可信的检索和读者权衡评估基础。

Abstract: Grounded language-model pipelines can be divided into three stages: selecting an object, retrieving passages for it, and using that evidence to answer. If the selected object must reach the reader, losing it breaks the handoff. Benchmark recall checks the dataset-linked object, which can differ. We audit 600 HybridQA questions across three selector families. On 1,463 resolvable records where the selected object matches the dataset-traced passage, exact key lookup and exact title matching return the object every time. With every ranked rule given the same decoded selected title, body-only BM25 omits it on 389 records (26.6%) at cutoff five, while hybrid retrieval with reranking omits it on 14 (1.0%). The two identities differ on 329 of 1,792 resolvable records. With original-question rankings, their top-five checks disagree on 106 records (5.9%). Frozen reader comparisons associate the aligned object's presence with 28.6 to 31.0 points higher exact match. In a deliberately selected 64-item cohort, removing that passage sharply lowers exact match, while removing a similar-length comparison passage does not reproduce the drop. We release the Returned-Object Profile (ROP), an executable record of the target, returned-ID field, cutoff, membership rule, and complete expected population, with data and an offline replay.

</details>


### [236] [$τ^τ$-Bench: An Environment for End-To-End, Realistic Agent Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04611)
*Quan Shi,Keshav Dhandhania,Karthik Narasimhan,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ττ-bench基准测试，将"智能体构建"本身作为任务——开发者智能体需要在真实业务记录、客户需求、生产API、继承代码库及服务成本约束下，交付一个完整的客服智能体，并通过部署该智能体与模拟用户交互来评分。实验显示最强配置（Claude Opus 5 + Claude Code）仅通过23.9%的模拟测试，而专家参考上限为82.2%，失败模式与人类开发者常见问题一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的编码智能体基准测试很少评估AI系统能否在真实客户参与条件下交付一个完整可用的智能体应用，而实际生产中LLM智能体正在被大规模部署，智能体的构建工作正越来越多地交由编码智能体完成。

Method: 构建ττ-bench基准：提供开发者智能体与真实业务一致的数据（业务记录、客户需求、生产API、待继承代码库、服务成本与模型限制），要求其交付完整客服智能体，通过部署该智能体与留出模拟用户进行交互来评分。共53个任务，覆盖4个领域。

Result: 最强配置（Claude Opus 5 + Claude Code）仅通过23.9%的评估模拟，而专家撰写的参考方案可达82.2%。失败模式包括：模型发出浅层查询而非深入理解记录、与客户沟通极少、对智能体架构和服务支出实验不足，往往直接上线首个能运行的方案。

Conclusion: ττ-bench旨在将"协作式智能体构建"转变为编码智能体的可量化目标，揭示当前模型在真实客户交付场景下的显著能力差距。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly becoming production software, deployed to handle customer service, adjudicate disputes, and operate internal systems. Notably, the work of building them is increasingly handed to coding agents, yet existing benchmarks say little about whether an AI system can deliver one under the conditions of a real client engagement. We introduce $τ^τ$-bench (pronounced hyper-tau-bench), a benchmark that makes agent construction the task. A developer agent is given the records a business actually keeps, a client who holds requirements, a production API that operations must run through, a codebase to inherit, and limits on serving cost and models: the same starting point a real engagement provides. From these it must deliver a complete customer-service agent, scored by deploying that agent against held-out simulated users. Across 53 tasks spanning four domains, the strongest configuration, Claude Opus 5 under Claude Code, passes just 23.9% of evaluation simulations. Meanwhile, an expert-authored reference ceiling scores 82.2%. The failures mirror ones human agent developers see: models issue shallow queries in place of deep comprehension of the records, communicate almost nothing to the client, and experiment too little with agent architecture and serving spend, shipping the first design that runs. We aim for $τ^τ$-bench to turn the work of cooperative agent building into a measurable target for coding agents.

</details>


### [237] [Leveraging Imperfect Restoration for Data Availability Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04627)
*Yi Huang,Jeremy Styborski,Mingzhi Lyu,Fan Wang,Adams Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IRP（Imperfect Restoration Poisoning）的新型数据投毒方法，旨在解决现有数据可用性攻击（DAA）在图像质量与投毒效果之间的权衡问题，并通过理论分析和大量实验验证了其在监督学习与自监督学习场景下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线数据的广泛使用面临被深度学习模型未经授权训练的威胁，现有DAA方法通常只在监督学习或自监督学习中表现优异，虽然CUDA方法在两个场景下都较为鲁棒，但其在自监督学习上的效果不佳，且图像质量与投毒效果之间存在严重的权衡问题。

Method: 本文首先对CUDA进行理论分析，揭示其引入的次优梯度以及利用类间偏差进行数据投毒的策略；在此基础上，提出一种名为Imperfect Restoration Poisoning（IRP）的投毒方法，旨在保持高图像质量的同时实现强投毒效果。

Result: 通过与八个基线方法在监督学习和自监督学习两大场景下的广泛比较，以及与五种代表性防御方法的联合评估，验证了IRP方法的优越性。

Conclusion: IRP在图像质量与投毒效果之间实现了更好的平衡，在监督学习和自监督学习场景下均表现出优于现有方法的效果，为数据可用性攻击提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: The abundance of online data is at risk of unauthorized usage in training deep learning models. To counter this, various Data Availability Attacks (DAAs) have been devised to make data unlearnable for such models by subtly perturbing the training data. However, existing attacks often excel against either Supervised Learning (SL) or Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) scenarios. Among these, a model-free approach that generates a Convolution-based Unlearnable Dataset (CUDA) stands out as the most robust DAA across both SSL and SL. Nonetheless, CUDA's effectiveness against SSL is underwhelming and it faces a severe trade-off between image quality and its poisoning effect. In this paper, we conduct a theoretical analysis of CUDA, uncovering the sub-optimal gradients it introduces and elucidating the strategy it employs to induce class-wise bias for data poisoning. Building on this, we propose a novel poisoning method named Imperfect Restoration Poisoning (IRP), aiming to preserve high image quality while achieving strong poisoning effects. Through extensive comparisons of IRP with eight baselines across SL and SSL, coupled with evaluations alongside five representative defense methods, we showcase the superiority of IRP. Code: https://github.com/lyumingzhi/IRP

</details>


### [238] [SiLR: Structure-Preserving Admission and Process Reward for LLM Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04629)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SiLR，通过在分支级别违规状态下使用乘积序来门控LLM工具代理的恢复性提案，证明任何标量替代都无法替代该序，并在多个基准上超越标量门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM工具代理的运行时门控常被建模为标量聚合分数的过滤器，但存在"标量投影陷阱"——接受局部改进的提案会将轨迹困在平台期，无法在违规状态下实现真正的恢复性进展。

Method: SiLR采用影子执行每个提案，并在"过载分支支持数"和"每分支严重度"上施加乘积序来判定是否接纳提案；通过确定性仿真将LLM置于信任边界之外，从而防御幅值重分配攻击。

Result: 在Gym-ANM场景中，SiLR恢复21/21多动作片段，终端基线0/21，最佳标量门控仅9/21；在CityLearn等多个模型族中一致验证；作为GRPO过程奖励时在所有挖掘场景中优于计数投影。

Conclusion: 标量投影在门控设计和奖励设计两个层面都丢失了违规几何结构，只有完整的分支级乘积序在结构性上是充分的，且能有效绕过标量与支持度纯基线的攻击防御。

Abstract: A runtime gate for an LLM tool agent is usually cast as a filter. In a ReAct loop a rejected proposal is followed by another at the same state, so the gate is a search operator over the proposal stream whose admission criterion shapes which trajectories are reachable. We study post-violation recovery admission, where progress must be admitted while the system is still in violation, and identify the scalar projection trap: an aggregate-score gate accepts a locally improving proposal and commits the trajectory to a plateau. SiLR instead shadow-executes each proposal and admits it under a product order over the branch-level violation state (overloaded-branch support and per-branch severity). We prove that no scalar surrogate is sound for this order, so the failure is representational, not a matter of threshold tuning. On mined Gym-ANM scenarios, SiLR recovers 21/21 multi-action episodes against 0/21 for terminal and 9/21 for the best scalar gate, significant across the full 24-scenario benchmark. The terminal-versus-structured dichotomy holds across three model families and in CityLearn. Because admission rests on deterministic simulation, the LLM lies outside the trust boundary: a magnitude-redistribution attack that defeats both scalar and support-only baselines is contained only by the full per-branch predicate. With two constraint families active, every tested scalar projection admits physically unsafe actions; support-only admits the largest fraction (63.2% of 42,410; product order 0). In the hardest dual-family traces, scalar gates recover only through that unsafe class. Reused as a GRPO process reward, it outperforms its count projection in every mined scenario and is the only tested reward whose ungated policy exceeds the untrained base (0.844 vs. 0.778). Scalar projection loses the violation geometry at both design points; only the full product order is structurally sufficient.

</details>


### [239] [A Cost-Aware Agentic Architecture for NL-to-SQL over Nested Enterprise Schemas, with a New Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04641)
*Yoga Sri Varshan Varadharajan,Ajay Yadav,Ritesh Goru,Prateek Chaudhury,Constantine Caramanis,Prateek Jain,Divyateja Pasupuleti,Sunil Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对企业级生产环境表结构的复杂性（图形化、半结构化、深层嵌套），提出了DevRev NL2SQL基准测试（含900条执行验证查询和语义深度评分SDS），并设计了成本感知的单次生成智能体架构。系统在DevRev基准上达到91.7%的答案正确率，比次优基线高出54.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然语言转SQL系统在学术基准上表现良好，但企业级生产表结构具有图形化、半结构化和深层嵌套的特征，现有基准无法有效衡量这些真实场景的需求。

Method: 提出两个互补贡献：(1) DevRev NL2SQL基准，包含900条执行验证查询，具备嵌套类型和链接图结构，并配套提出与模式无关的语义深度评分SDS；(2) 设计成本感知的单次生成智能体架构，其中模式选择、元数据检索和错误修复组件均针对该场景需求进行专门设计。

Result: 在DevRev NL2SQL基准上，系统达到91.7%的答案正确率，比次优基线高出54.6个百分点；在Spider 2.0 Snowflake公开数据集上与领先系统相当，且仅需单次生成操作点。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过专门针对复杂企业级模式设计的基准测试和架构优化，自然语言转SQL系统可以在真实生产场景中实现显著优于现有基线的性能，同时保持成本效益。

Abstract: Natural-language-to-SQL systems have ad- vanced rapidly on academic benchmarks, yet production enterprise schemas exhibit graph- like, semi-structured, deeply nested structure that current benchmarks do not measure. We make two complementary contributions. First, we introduce the DevRev NL2SQL bench- mark: 900 execution-verified queries with nested-type and link-graph structure, accom- panied by the Semantic Depth Score (SDS), a schema-agnostic rubric for analytical reasoning depth. Second, we present a cost-aware single- generation agentic architecture whose schema- selection, metadata-retrieval, and error-repair components are designed for the requirements this regime imposes. On the DevRev NL2SQL benchmark the system attains 91.7% answer correctness, a margin of 54.6 percentage points over the next-best baseline; on the Spider 2.0 Snowflake public dataset, it is competitive with leading systems at a single-generation operating point.

</details>


### [240] [Continual Graph Memory for Adaptive Recommendation under Intent Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04651)
*Hao Nguyen Ngoc,Tung Nguyen,Nguyen Thi Hanh,Hoang Thai Dinh,Nguyen Xuan Tung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CGM-Rec，一个用于自适应推荐的连续图记忆框架。它将知识图谱视为可写内存，通过语义图记忆（质量门控的保守更新存储高置信关系知识）和情景教训记忆（快速反应式学习近期结果和失败案例）两个互补组件来处理意图漂移问题。测试阶段模型参数冻结，仅通过内存写入实现自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统面临意图漂移挑战时，传统KG增强系统将图视为静态检索基板，难以应对不断变化的用户意图、嘈杂元数据和重复失败模式，缺乏适应性和灵活性。

Method: CGM-Rec包含两个组件：(1) 语义图记忆——通过质量门控的类型化操作进行保守更新，存储稳定且高置信的关系知识；(2) 情景教训记忆——作为快速反应式记忆，学习近期结果、失败案例和纠正提示。测试阶段模型参数冻结，仅通过内存写入实现自适应。

Result: 在多种推荐设置下，CGM-Rec在大多数指标上优于神经方法和基于LLM的基线。采样候选重排下，在Bundle数据集上HR@1较最强LLM基线提升29.58%；在ML-100K上HR@5达0.5941（K-RagRec为0.4746）。

Conclusion: CGM-Rec通过将知识图谱转化为可写记忆并结合慢（语义）与快（情景）双重记忆机制，在参数冻结条件下仍能实现有效的自适应推荐，验证了持续图记忆方案在意图漂移场景下的优越性。

Abstract: This paper studies adaptive recommendation under intent drift, where feedback from each recommendation outcome can reveal whether the relational evidence used for ranking is useful, missing, or misleading.
